We prove that inside this set the mixing step can reduce the norm of the residual streams only by shrinking the differences between the streams, while their mean is left unchanged; and since the reduction accumulates over layers, the streams grow more alike and their diversity is spent with depth. We therefore propose Orthogonal Hyper-Connections (oHC), restricting the residual matrix to the rotation group $SO(n)$, so that the mixing step can neither amplify nor attenuate the residual streams in any direction, which keeps training stable and no longer forces the differences between the streams to contract. Specifically, at the four streams used by recent HC models we parameterize the group in closed form by a pair of unit quaternions, which adds no parameters, replaces the iterative projection with a fixed pattern of signed additions, and can be constructed faster than mHC. We evaluate oHC across a comprehensive set of downstream tasks, where it outperforms the single-stream residual baseline, mHC and iHC, which fixes the residual matrix to the identity.

</details>


### [168] [From Tokens to Semantics: Leveraging Complementary Signals for Hallucination Detection in Black-Box LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02679)
*Urja Pawar,Rajitha Ramanayake,Owen O'Neill,Nabeel Kemal,Abhishek Mandal,Houssem Chatbri,Christopher Martin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在无可信上下文或参考文档的情况下，利用黑盒模型API中的两种信号——语义熵（衡量采样响应意义的分歧）和基于标记概率的不确定性——来检测大语言模型生成内容中的错误。针对两者各自失效场景（语义熵在响应集中时失效，而令牌不确定性可能忽略一致但错误的自信输出），作者提出改进方法：通过TopK聚合多响应的令牌级信号以增强不确定性检测；设计混合方法CoCoA，结合目标响应不确定性与语义差异性；并提出两种监督学习方法——Gated（将单簇情况导向聚合令牌特征分类器）和Stacked（联合学习语义不确定性和更广泛的令牌特征）。在七个基准测试上评估了四种语言模型的表现，结果显示Stacked在近一半情况下表现最佳，而TopK和CoCoA无需标注数据且保持竞争力，但需精细校准阈值。没有单一方法在所有场景下最优，因此进一步在1%至15%假阳性率预算下评估性能，分析对生成与校准选择的敏感性，并考察不同数据集特性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当大语言模型应用于公开或高风险工作流时，遗漏的虚假生成可能损害用户和机构，而误报则浪费有限的人工审核资源。在缺乏可信上下文或参考文档的场景下，如何有效检测生成内容中的错误成为关键挑战。现有方法依赖的语义熵和令牌不确定性各有局限，亟需互补性机制提升检测鲁棒性。

Method: 提出三种核心方法：1) TopK方法，通过聚合多轮采样响应中的令牌级信号增强不确定性检测；2) CoCoA方法，融合目标响应不确定性与语义不相似性；3) 两种监督学习方法：Gated通过路由策略处理单语义簇情形，使分类器专注于聚合令牌特征；Stacked则联合学习语义不确定性与广泛令牌特征，实现端到端优化。

Result: 在七项基准测试（包括四个文本数据集、一个多模态手写支票提取任务，以及两个自建数据集：金融摘要与长文本问答）中，四种语言模型的实验表明：Stacked方法在近半数情况下表现最佳；TopK与CoCoA虽无需标注数据，但需仔细校准阈值，仍具竞争力。整体上无方法普遍最优。进一步分析显示，在1%-15%假阳性率预算内，各方法性能受生成策略和校准方式影响显著，且随数据集特性变化而波动。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了当前大语言模型生成质量检测方法的局限性与互补潜力。提出的方法在不同条件下各有优势，强调了根据具体应用场景和资源约束选择合适检测策略的重要性。未来工作应聚焦于降低对人工标注的依赖，探索更鲁棒的无监督/弱监督框架，并深入理解生成行为与检测信号之间的关系。

Abstract: When LLMs support public-facing or high-stakes workflows, missed fabrications can harm users and institutions, while false alarms consume limited human-review capacity. When no trusted context or reference document is available, we study two signals accessible through black-box model APIs: semantic entropy, which measures disagreement among sampled response meanings, and uncertainty derived from token log-probabilities. Their failure modes can be complementary: semantic entropy becomes uninformative when responses form one semantic cluster, while token uncertainty can miss consistently confident errors. We extend token-based uncertainty detection by aggregating token-level signals across sampled responses through our TopK method, evaluate the hybrid CoCoA method, which combines target-response uncertainty with semantic dissimilarity, and propose and study two supervised methods: Gated, which routes single-cluster cases to an aggregated-token-feature classifier, and Stacked, which learns jointly from semantic uncertainty and broader token features. We evaluate seven benchmarks, including five public benchmarks (four text datasets and multimodal handwritten-cheque extraction) and two constructed benchmarks (Financial Summaries and Long-Text QA), using four language models. In our evaluation across models and datasets, Stacked gave the best performance in nearly half of the cases, while TopK and CoCoA remain competitive without supervised training labels, although their thresholds require careful calibration. No method is universally strongest. We therefore evaluate performance at false-positive-rate budgets from 1% to 15%, assess their sensitivity to generation and calibration choices, and examine variation across dataset characteristics.

</details>


### [169] [DKL: Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02685)
*Kushagra Bhushan,Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Gaurav Pandey,Sonam Gupta,Vineet Kumar,Jaydeep Sen,Yatin Nandwani,Sachindra Joshi,Dinesh Raghu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DKL提出一种轻量级方法，通过在基础语言模型上进行扩展预训练并合并权重，将新知识注入指令微调的语言模型中，避免了昂贵的指令微调，同时保持其指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法在检索失败时容易产生幻觉；虽然微调方法如RAFT和PA-RAG能增强RAG，但需要大量合成数据；而扩展预训练虽无需合成数据，却损害了指令遵循能力，且后续指令微调成本高且可能不可行。

Method: DKL在基础语言模型上执行扩展预训练以注入新知识，再通过模型合并技术将知识权重迁移至指令微调的语言模型中，从而在不破坏其指令遵循能力的前提下提升知识获取能力。

Result: 实验表明，DKL在检索失败情况下将RAG准确率从54.17%提升至79.26%，优于先前方法，且使用更少的训练数据。

Conclusion: DKL是一种高效、轻量的方法，能够在不依赖昂贵指令微调的情况下有效增强指令微调语言模型的知识能力，适用于缺乏指令微调数据的场景。

Abstract: RAG has become the de facto method for incorporating new, corpus-specific knowledge into an instruction following LLM (Instruct LLM). Although RAG-based prompting improves factual grounding, it fails when retrieval is incorrect or incomplete, leading to hallucinations. Finetuning methods such as RAFT and PA-RAG enhance RAG by injecting new knowledge into the model's parameters, but require generating a massive amount of synthetic QA that covers the entire corpus. Extended Pre-Training (EPT) on the text corpus avoids the need for comprehensive synthetic data generation but compromises an Instruct LLM's instruction-following capabilities, necessitating instruction fine-tuning (IFT) after pre-training. However, IFT is costly and may be infeasible due to the unavailability of an instruction-tuning corpus. In this work, we propose DKL-Decoupled Knowledge Learning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models. Instead of doing EPT on the Instruct LLM, DKL performs EPT on its corresponding base LLM to infuse new knowledge. These knowledge infused weights are then merged with the Instruct LLM, imparting new knowledge without affecting their instruction-following capabilities. DKL is a lightweight method that avoids expensive instruction fine-tuning and relies on model merging to infuse the new knowledge into the Instruct LLM without destroying its instruction following capabilities. Empirical results show that DKL improves RAG accuracy from 54.17 to 79.26 on retrieval failure cases, while outperforming prior approaches with substantially less training data.

</details>


### [170] [Trace as State: Reasoning Traces as Conditional States for Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02702)
*Xu Zou,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出将推理轨迹（Trace）作为状态信息前置到长上下文处理前，以改善因果Transformer在长上下文推理中的表现。通过对比实验，在多个模型和数据集上，该方法显著优于将轨迹置于末尾的对照组（Trace Append），尤其在复杂推理任务中提升明显，证明了前置状态信息的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果Transformer在处理长上下文时，若关键条件信息延迟提供，可能导致内存需求呈指数级增长。因此需要一种机制，使早期推导的信息能有效指导后续重读过程。

Method: 提出‘Trace as State’方法，将收集的推理轨迹作为文本代理的状态信息，放置于长上下文块之前进行一次新遍历，从而让前期获得的信息引导后续的重新阅读与推理。

Result: 在三个模型和三个长上下文数据集上的27个组合中，有26个结果表明Trace as State优于对照组；例如在GraphWalks Parents任务中，DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview的准确率从29.2%提升至81.8%，GLM-5.2达到100%。

Conclusion: 将推理轨迹作为状态信息前置，可显著提升长上下文推理性能，同时保持因果Transformer的结构不变，是一种高效且实用的方法。

Abstract: Transformers process information causally, but long-context reasoning may depend on task state discovered only later. We formalize this mismatch through conditional state update tasks. For causal state update processors, providing the condition first can require exponentially less memory in the worst case than providing it last.
  Motivated by this principle, we introduce Trace as State. We use collected reasoning traces as a textual proxy for task state and place it before the long-context block on a fresh pass, allowing information derived previously to guide rereading.
  We conduct extensive experiments on Trace as State and Trace Append, a matched control that uses the same task state proxy but put it after the context. Across three models and three long-context datasets, Trace as State outperforms Trace Append in 26 of 27 reported combinations of model, task, and metric. On GraphWalks Parents, exact match lifts DeepSeek V4 Pro Preview from 29.2% on the initial pass and 43.0% with Trace Appendto 81.8% with Trace as State, and from 66.4% and 83.2% to 100.0% for GLM-5.2. These results show that placing traces before the context can improve long-context reasoning while retaining the causal transformer structure.

</details>


### [171] [CORAL: An LLM-Native Harness for Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02730)
*Muhammad Rafay Azhar,Yuhang Zhou,Gilbert Jiang,Yuchen Wang,Rahul Sharma,Matthew DeSousa,Jiayi Liu,Xin Guo,Lizhu Zhang,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CORAL 是一个基于大语言模型的闭环推荐系统优化框架，通过智能体持续观察系统信号、结合历史决策与结果进行推理，并利用工具（如数值优化器）在固定预算内调整推荐策略。该方法将推荐优化建模为部分可观测、非平稳且有约束的优化问题，无需参数更新即可从过往行为中学习并改进策略。在两个大型社交平台上的A/B实验表明，CORAL能在不增加服务成本的情况下提升用户参与度，或在不降低参与度的前提下降低服务成本，有效覆盖了参与度-效率权衡边界。随着迭代次数增加，性能持续提升，证明单一智能体循环可自动化传统上由人工算法工程师完成的持续优化工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统的优化依赖人工工程师通过在线实验进行，过程缓慢且受限于工程资源，难以应对内容、用户行为和上游模型的动态变化。尽管大语言模型已在排序、用户建模等方面应用，但缺乏能持续闭环运行、自主调整并从实际效果中学习的智能体系统。因此，亟需一种能够自动、持续优化推荐系统配置的新范式。

Method: 提出CORAL框架，采用大语言模型作为核心智能体，每轮周期中：1）观测实时运行信号；2）结合记忆中的历史决策与结果进行推理；3）调用工具（包括数值优化器）调整推荐系统配置，确保在固定运营预算内；4）根据实际测量结果反馈至下一轮决策，形成闭环学习机制。整个过程被视为一个部分可观测、非平稳、有约束的优化问题，策略通过上下文学习不断改进，而非依赖模型参数更新。

Result: 在两个大规模社交平台的A/B实验中，同一套CORAL框架分别实现了：1）在无额外服务成本前提下提升用户参与度；2）在不损害用户参与度前提下降低服务成本。性能随迭代次数增长，验证了该框架在覆盖参与度-效率权衡边界方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CORAL证明了基于大语言模型的智能体闭环系统能够在真实生产环境中实现持续、自动化、安全的推荐优化，显著减少对人工干预的依赖，具备广泛部署潜力。

Abstract: Production recommender systems shape what billions of people see, and sustaining their performance requires continual optimization: as content, user behavior, and upstream models shift, the choices governing retrieval, ranking, and serving must be revisited. Traditionally, human engineers test such changes through online experiments--a slow, reactive process limited by engineering effort, leaving parts of the system unrevised as conditions change. Although large language models have been applied to ranking, user modeling, and offline model development, few systems place an agent in a continual closed loop that acts on a live recommender and learns from the measured effects of its decisions. We present CORAL (Constraint-Optimized Recommender via an Agentic Loop), an LLM-native harness that closes this loop: each cycle, the agent observes operating signals, reasons over a memory of past decisions and outcomes, and invokes tools--including a numerical optimizer that keeps changes within a fixed operating budget--to reconfigure the recommender, with measured outcomes informing the next cycle. We formulate this as a partially observed, non-stationary, constrained optimization problem in which the policy improves in context, without parameter updates, from its prior actions. Across two large-scale social platforms, evaluated with A/B experiments, the same harness improves engagement at no additional serving cost on one and reduces serving cost without degrading engagement on the other, spanning the engagement-efficiency frontier. Performance improves as the loop iterates, suggesting that a single agentic loop can automate continual optimization work traditionally performed by human algorithm engineers under explicit guardrails.

</details>


### [172] [Choosing a PEFT Variant for Per-Patient Dysarthric ASR: A Single-Speaker Case Study on Two ASR Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02735)
*Bernard Muller,László Tóth,LaVonne Roberts*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在单个患者（严重中风后构音障碍的匈牙利男性）的语音识别场景下，对比了七种LoRA家族方法（LoRA、QLoRA、AdaLoRA、DoRA、LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA）在两个主流ASR模型（Whisper-large-v3和多语言Qwen3-ASR-1.7B）上的表现。结果表明，注意力投影适配器显著降低词错误率（CER），其中LoRA与DoRA性能无显著差异（p>0.5），但LoRA更简单且成本更低，被选为首选；4位量化QLoRA表现更差且未节省内存；其他方法均不及LoRA家族，尽管LoHA仍带来18.6%的相对CER改善。全微调性能最优（11.43% CER），但仅需115MB的LoRA适配器（含前馈层）即可达到其99.34%的精度，存储开销仅为3.7%。6点注册测试显示，约5分钟音频可捕获45.6%的零样本到30分钟的性能提升，后续在10和30分钟仍有增益（限于单一患者、语言及严重构音障碍）。训练代码与配置将公开供研究使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在个体化构音障碍自动语音识别（ASR）中，虽然每患者适配器是主流生产架构，但参数高效微调（PEFT）方法在说话人依赖的单患者场景下的比较尚不充分。本研究旨在系统评估多种LoRA变体在真实临床患者数据上的有效性，以指导高效、低成本的个性化模型部署。

Method: 采用单个严重中风后构音障碍患者（S1，409条语音）的数据，在Whisper-large-v3（匈牙利语微调）和Qwen3-ASR-1.7B两个基础模型上，对七种LoRA家族方法进行对比实验。通过三组随机种子进行配对自助法检验性能差异，并评估不同注册音频长度（6点网格）对性能的影响。同时对比全微调与轻量级适配器的精度与存储开销。

Result: 注意力投影适配器显著降低CER；LoRA与DoRA性能无显著差异（p>0.5），LoRA因更简单而被采纳；4位量化QLoRA表现更差且无内存优势；其他方法（LoHA、VeRA、VB-LoRA、AdaLoRA）均未超越LoRA；全微调最佳（11.43% CER），但115MB的LoRA适配器（含前馈层）仅损失0.66个百分点，存储仅为3.7%；5分钟音频可捕获45.6%的性能提升，10和30分钟进一步优化。

Conclusion: 在严重构音障碍患者的个性化ASR任务中，推荐使用简单的LoRA适配器，因其在精度与成本之间取得最佳平衡。少量注册语音（如5分钟）即可实现大部分性能提升，支持高效部署。相关训练脚本与配置将在发表后开源供研究使用。

Abstract: Per-patient adapters are the preferred production architecture for dysarthric automatic speech recognition (ASR), yet parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) variants have not been compared in the speaker-dependent, per-patient regime. We present a single-speaker case study comparing seven LoRA-family methods (LoRA, QLoRA, AdaLoRA, DoRA, LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA) on two production bases (Whisper-large-v3 with Hungarian fine-tuning, and a multilingual Qwen3-ASR-1.7B checkpoint) for one post-stroke Hungarian male speaker (S1, 409 utterances; severe dysarthria on auditory-perceptual clinical assessment). Attention-projection adapters substantially improve CER on both bases. Across three seeds, a paired bootstrap detects no significant LoRA-DoRA difference (p>0.5; 13.86/13.90 % CER on Whisper, 28.10/28.33 % on Qwen3-ASR), so we adopt the simpler, cheaper LoRA. Real 4-bit (NF4) QLoRA is worse on every seed and both bases (14.56/30.09 % CER) with no memory saving at this scale, and LoHA, VeRA, VB-LoRA and AdaLoRA do not reach the LoRA family, though LoHA still gives an 18.6 % relative CER reduction on Whisper. On the same base, full fine-tuning is more accurate (11.43 % CER), but a 115 MB LoRA that also adapts the feed-forward blocks reaches within 0.66 pp of it at approximately 3.7 % of the per-patient storage. A 6-point enrollment grid shows about 5 min of patient audio captures 45.6 % of the zero-shot-to-30-min CER reduction, with further gains at 10 and 30 min (caveat: one speaker, one language, severe post-stroke dysarthria). Training scripts and recipes will be released, source-available under a research-use licence, on publication.

</details>


### [173] [Language Models Can Control Their Own Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02737)
*Namgyu Ho,Huzama Ahmad,Woosung Koh,Se-Young Yun,Tal Schuster,Cicero Nogueira dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Declarative Attention (DA) is introduced to reduce the computational cost of attention in large language models by allowing the model to declare where it needs to attend during generation, thus avoiding full KV cache reads. It operates in three modes: <global> (full context), <focus> (specific region), and <local> (recent output only). This intrinsic method significantly reduces attended tokens (52.0%, 31.1%) with small accuracy drops that decrease with larger models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods rely on extrinsic proxy scores to select relevant tokens, which still incur O(N) cost per step. The authors question whether models already know what parts of the context are relevant, leading to an intrinsic approach.

Method: Declarative Attention (DA) enables models to declare their attention needs via a protocol interpreted by the inference engine, which skips unnecessary KV cache reads based on these declarations.

Result: On off-the-shelf models (Gemma-4-31B, Qwen-3.6-27B), DA reduces total attended tokens by 52.0% and 31.1% respectively, with only modest accuracy drops (1.27pp, 2.75pp) that diminish as model scale increases.

Conclusion: DA introduces a new axis for sparse attention through intrinsic model declarations, offering significant efficiency gains and opening avenues for future training-based improvements.

Abstract: Language models spend most of their attention on a small fraction of context, yet they read the entire KV cache to find the few tokens that matter. If the user asks about a previous detail in a 1M-token conversation, global attention layers must scan the full context to generate each token of the reply. A prominent approach mitigates this cost by pre-selecting relevant tokens via lightweight proxy scores, but this extrinsic scoring still incurs O(N) per step. We take an intrinsic approach motivated by the simple question: wouldn't the model already know which parts of the context are relevant? To this end, we introduce Declarative Attention (DA), a protocol that elicits the model to declare where it needs to attend within its chain-of-thought, partitioning generation into three modes: <global> (full context), <focus> (a specific region), and <local> (recent output only). The inference engine parses these declarations like tool calls and skips most of the KV cache read. Under zero-shot evaluation across 15 long-context tasks, DA on off-the-shelf models (Gemma-4-31B, Qwen-3.6-27B) significantly reduces total attended tokens during decoding (52.0%, 31.1%) with modest accuracy drops (1.27pp, 2.75pp) that shrink with model scale. DA unlocks a new axis of sparse attention, with further potential under training-based methods that future work can explore.

</details>


### [174] [Untangling the Mechanisms of Misleading Context in Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02754)
*Robin Linzmayer,Noémie Elhadad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型在医疗问答中因误导性上下文导致判断偏差的问题。在MedMisBench基准上，通过注入伪造证据和单纯断言两类误导信息，发现模型对断言更敏感，采纳率高出10至27个百分点。尽管误导信息在推理轨迹中披露率高达81%-98%，但在最终回答中仅7%-90%被披露，且断言类误导的披露率低于证据类。分析显示，伪造证据早期进入并逐步累积影响，而断言则在推理末期直接扭转结论。使用大模型监控器结合开放推理轨迹可检测78%的错误决策，误报率仅5%，远优于仅依赖输出结果的32%上限。因此，最易导致模型出错的误导信息最难被披露，且只有通过开放推理轨迹才能可靠检测，而当前前沿模型通常不提供此类信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在医疗问答中表现接近专家水平，但其判断可能受误导性上下文影响。理解这些误导如何破坏模型推理、是否可被检测，对于保障医疗AI的安全性和可靠性至关重要。现有模型多隐藏内部推理过程，限制了对错误根源的分析与监控能力。

Method: 在医学推理子集MedMisBench（共8,627个问题）上，引入两类误导性上下文：伪造证据和纯断言。测试三种推理模型——两种公开完整推理过程的模型和一种仅输出最终答案的前沿模型。通过分析推理轨迹与输出响应中的信息披露情况，对比不同误导类型的影响；利用重采样方法分析推理路径差异；构建基于大模型的监控系统，评估其在读取推理轨迹时的检测能力。

Result: 模型对断言类误导比对伪造证据更敏感，采纳率高出10-27个百分点；误导信息在推理轨迹中披露率高（81%-98%），但在最终回答中披露率低（7%-90%），且断言类披露率低于证据类；伪造证据在推理早期介入并持续积累影响，断言则在后期直接改变结论方向；基于开放推理轨迹的监控系统可捕获78%的错误决策，误报率仅5%，显著优于仅依赖输出的32%上限。

Conclusion: 最危险的误导性上下文（即断言）往往最难被揭示，且只有通过访问开放的推理轨迹才能有效检测。当前主流前沿模型隐藏推理过程的做法，严重削弱了对错误决策的可监控性，制约了医疗AI系统的可信度与安全性。应推动可解释性与透明性机制建设，以保障高风险场景下的模型可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models now answer medical questions with expert-level performance. However, the context these systems act on can be misleading, and misleading context can corrupt a model's medical judgment. To understand how misleading context corrupts this judgment, we examine the model's susceptibility to the context, disclosure of it, mechanism of corrupted reasoning, and monitorability of the decision. On the medical reasoning subset of MedMisBench, a clinician-reviewed question-answering benchmark of 8,627 questions, we inject two types of misleading context cues, fabricated evidence and a bare assertion. We test three reasoning models, two that expose their full reasoning trace and one frontier model that exposes only its response. All three are more susceptible to the assertion than to the fabricated evidence, adopting the asserted answer 10 to 27 points more often. The misleading cues are disclosed in 81 to 98% of traces but only 7 to 90% of responses, and the assertion is disclosed less often than evidence based cues. Resampling from reasoning traces without disclosure shows the two cues corrupt reasoning differently, evidence entering early and accumulating while the assertion redirects the conclusion near its end. An LLM monitor catches 78% of corrupted decisions at 5% false positives when reading an open model's trace with guidance, against at most 32% from any response. The misleading context that models are most susceptible to is disclosed least, and was caught reliably only from an open reasoning trace, which frontier providers withhold.

</details>


### [175] [From Reweighting to Rewriting: Unlocking the Intervention Effects of Influential Samples in Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02771)
*Yuzhang Luo,Chenpeng Wang,Jianhui Chen,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于影响函数（IF）的响应重写方法，用于提升训练数据归因（TDA）在模型干预中的有效性。与传统的权重重调相比，响应重写在相同选中的示例上能产生更强、更持久且可双向调控的行为变化，表明影响函数识别出的示例具有更大的干预潜力，而传统重调无法充分释放这种潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有影响函数（IF）虽能识别对模型行为有显著影响的训练样本，但基于权重调整的干预效果有限且不一致，这引发疑问：是这些样本本身缺乏干预价值，还是重调方式未能发挥其潜在作用？

Method: 引入影响引导的响应重写方法，利用影响函数筛选干预目标，并在保持指令不变的前提下，将目标样本的输出替换为与目标行为一致或相反的监督信号。

Result: 在四个开源大模型上，响应重写相较于重调在知识性回避和安全拒绝任务中均表现出更强、更持久且可逆的行为改变；影响选择的样本在重写下展现出更高杠杆效应，且行为变化集中于目标相关领域。

Conclusion: 影响函数所识别的样本具备显著的干预潜力，但传统重调无法有效利用该潜力；应发展面向干预能力的评估框架以更准确衡量训练数据归因方法的价值。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) aims to identify training examples that shape model behavior, but its intervention value depends on both which examples are selected and how they are modified. Influence functions (IF) estimate behavioral changes under infinitesimal reweighting, yet IF-selected examples often show limited advantages over random selection under conventional weight-based interventions. This raises the question of whether influential examples lack intervention value or whether reweighting fails to realize their behavioral leverage.We introduce influence-guided response rewriting, which uses IF to identify intervention targets and replaces their responses with behavior-aligned or behavior-opposed supervision while keeping instructions fixed. Across four open-weight LLMs, we compare rewriting and reweighting on the same influence-selected examples using epistemic abstention as our primary testbed. Response rewriting produces stronger, more persistent, and bidirectional behavioral shifts, while reweighting the same examples yields weak and inconsistent effects. Further analyses show that influence-selected examples provide greater rewriting leverage than alternative selectors, with changes remaining concentrated on target-relevant behaviors. The same qualitative contrast extends to safety refusal. These results distinguish the local reweighting effects captured by influence estimates from the broader intervention leverage of the examples they identify, motivating intervention-aware evaluation of TDA methods.

</details>


### [176] [HyperStyler: Low-resource Authorship Style Transfer via Context-aware Style Navigation and Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02772)
*Jongkyung Shin,Minguk Jeon,Chanwoo Park,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyperStyler 是一种新型的低资源作者风格迁移方法，通过解耦风格选择与风格实现，在仅用少量参考样本的情况下，有效提升风格保真度和语义保留能力。其核心创新在于使用动态参数调制（Stylo-hypernet）替代传统的隐藏状态注入，并通过联合建模源上下文与目标作者参考样本来预测风格坐标（Stylo-navigator）。在 Reddit、Blog 和 News 数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有方法，包括基于大模型的方法，且参数增加仅 2.4%，推理速度超过 1.8 倍于大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低资源作者风格迁移方法因将多样参考样本压缩为单一静态作者嵌入，导致风格信息平均化，且依赖隐藏表示进行风格控制，造成风格与内容纠缠，难以同时保证风格保真度与语义一致性。

Method: 提出 HyperStyler 架构，将风格迁移分为风格选择（Stylo-navigator）与风格实现（Stylo-hypernet）两阶段。前者通过联合建模源文本上下文与目标作者参考样本来预测风格坐标；后者通过动态参数调制实现风格表达，避免隐藏状态注入带来的内容干扰。

Result: 在 Reddit、Blog、News 等数据集上，HyperStyler 在风格保真度和语义保留方面均显著优于现有方法，包括 LLM-based 模型。其额外参数仅增加 2.4%（相对于 T5-large），推理速度超过 1.8 倍于 LLMs，展现出高效与强泛化能力。

Conclusion: HyperStyler 成功实现了低资源场景下风格迁移的高保真与语义一致，通过解耦风格选择与实现机制，结合动态参数调制，显著提升了性能与效率，是当前最具竞争力的低资源风格迁移方法之一。

Abstract: Low-resource authorship style transfer (LAST) aims to rewrite text into the style of an arbitrary target author using only a few reference examples while preserving the original meaning. Existing methods often struggle to achieve both high style fidelity and semantic preservation because they compress diverse references into a single static author embedding, which averages out context-dependent stylistic variation, and rely on hidden representations for style control, which entangle style with content. We propose HyperStyler, a novel architecture that decouples LAST into style selection and style realization. Stylo-navigator predicts style coordinates by jointly modeling the source context and target-author references, and Stylo-hypernet realizes them via dynamic parameter modulation instead of hidden-state injection. Our experiments on Reddit, Blog, and News datasets demonstrate that HyperStyler consistently outperforms prior methods including LLM-based approaches and generalizes robustly across domains. Notably, HyperStyler achieves superior performance with as few as 2.4% additional parameters over T5-large, while being over 1.8x faster than LLMs at inference.

</details>


### [177] [EarlyEval: Cheaper Agent Evaluation via Early Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02783)
*Yuling Shi,Zhensu Sun,Junsen Dong,Chengcheng Wan,David Lo,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为EarlyEval的轻量级框架，通过早期结果预测技术，在不显著影响评估准确率的前提下，大幅降低大型语言模型（LLM）智能体评估的成本。其核心思想是：智能体的最终成败往往在执行过程中早期行为中已可判断。EarlyEval利用LightGBM训练成功与失败分类器，基于行为、文本和参考解特征，在达到置信度阈值时提前终止任务，从而减少13%-26%的执行步骤，并最多节省44.1%输入token和29.4%输出token，同时保持89%-97%的预测准确率，对每智能体解决率的影响仅为1-2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估成本高昂，单次运行前沿模型在代理基准上可能花费数百至数千美元，且反复迭代开发加剧开销。尽管已有研究通过基准蒸馏减少任务数量，但未降低每个任务的执行成本。因此亟需一种新方法，在不牺牲评估质量的前提下，从任务内部层面实现效率提升。

Method: 提出EarlyEval框架，通过训练一对LightGBM分类器（成功与失败），结合行为特征、文本特征及参考解特征，实时监测智能体执行过程中的中间状态。当任一分类器置信度超过校准阈值时，立即终止任务，实现早期预测与剪枝，仅引入极小的每步开销。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified、TerminalBench和Toolathlon三个基准上，EarlyEval可减少13%-26%的智能体执行步骤，最多节省44.1%输入token和29.4%输出token，预测准确率达89%-97%，对智能体解决率的平均扰动仅为1-2个百分点。

Conclusion: EarlyEval通过早期结果预测机制，为高效评估LLM智能体提供了有效且低成本的解决方案，显著降低了评估过程中的计算开销，同时保持了高精度与稳定性，为未来智能体开发的迭代优化提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM agents is essential for guiding their development, yet it has grown prohibitively expensive: a single pass of a frontier model over an agentic benchmark can cost hundreds to thousands of dollars, a price paid repeatedly across iterative development cycles. Prior efforts, centered on benchmark distillation, reduce the number of evaluation tasks but leave the cost of executing each retained task untouched. In this work, we introduce early outcome prediction, a complementary axis of efficiency that instead cuts cost within each task. Our key insight is that an agent's final outcome is often evident from its intermediate behavior well before execution completes. We instantiate this idea in EarlyEval, a lightweight framework that trains a pair of LightGBM success and failure classifiers over behavioral, textual, and reference-solution features, and halts an agent run the moment either classifier crosses a calibrated confidence threshold, adding negligible per-step overhead. Across three benchmarks, SWE-bench Verified, TerminalBench, and Toolathlon, EarlyEval can eliminate 13%-26% of agent steps and up to 44.1% input tokens and 29.4% output tokens at 89%-97% prediction accuracy, while perturbing per-agent resolve rates by only one to two percentage points on average.

</details>


### [178] [DiscoSign: Discourse-Aware Text to Sign Language Gloss Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02796)
*Vasileios Baltatzis,Mert Inan,Connor Gillis,Raja Kushalnagar,Lorna Quandt,Leah Findlater,Colin Lea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiscoSign，一种基于大语言模型的、注重语篇的文本到手语词汇翻译方法，旨在解决手语理解中的三大关键语篇现象：空间指代消解、问答从句（QACs）以及概念-词汇一致性。传统翻译评估指标无法衡量语篇层面的质量，因此引入新的评估指标来评估各维度的语篇连贯性。实验表明，该方法在空间一致性和实体追踪方面显著优于仅基于句子的翻译，同时保持了与现有单句翻译相当的性能。这是首个系统性的手语语篇级翻译框架及其评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手语处理系统仅在句子层面操作，忽略了对理解手语至关重要的语篇现象。为提升手语翻译的自然性和准确性，需引入语篇意识的处理机制。

Method: 提出一个模块化的大语言模型（LLM）基础翻译框架，专门处理空间指代消解、问答从句（QACs）和概念-词汇一致性三个核心语篇现象，并设计了一套新的评估指标以衡量语篇连贯性。

Result: 在句子级和语篇级数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在空间一致性和实体追踪方面显著优于仅基于句子的翻译，同时保持了高质量的单句翻译效果。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了首个系统性的语篇级文本到手语词汇翻译框架，并提供了相应的评估方法，为未来手语处理系统的语篇理解能力发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Sign language processing systems have traditionally operated at the sentence level, ignoring critical discourse phenomena fundamental to sign language comprehension. We introduce DiscoSign, a computational approach for discourse-aware text to sign language gloss translation grounded in linguistic research. We address three key phenomena within our modular Large Language Model (LLM)-based translation framework: (i) spatial coreference resolution, where entities maintain consistent spatial locations throughout discourse; (ii) Question-Answer Clauses (QACs), pseudocleft structures serving specific discourse functions; and (iii) concept-gloss consistency, ensuring stable mappings between English concepts and American Sign Language (ASL) signs. Traditional translation metrics fail to capture discourse-level quality, so we introduce a suite of novel evaluation metrics designed to assess each dimension of discourse coherence addressed by our framework. Experiments on sentence-level and discourse-level datasets show that our approach for discourse-aware processing significantly improves spatial consistency and entity tracking relative to sentence-only translation, while maintaining competitive single-sentence gloss translation quality. Our work establishes the first systematic framework for discourse-level text to sign language gloss translation with corresponding evaluation methodology.

</details>


### [179] [User Feedback Provides a Unique Signal that LLMs Can not Detect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02859)
*Shachar Don-Yehiya,Leshem Choshen,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了用户反馈在大语言模型中因噪声而无效的主流观点，指出其真正价值被现有评估范式中的系统性偏差所掩盖。通过构建具有明确真实答案的合成数据和自然数据，研究发现基于反馈的模型修订能显著更有效地解决特定问题。根本问题在于，当模型仅因反馈修正错误时，大语言模型裁判常无法识别真正修正的结果，反而偏好表现较差的基线输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究认为用户反馈噪声大、难以利用，但本文质疑这一观点，认为其有效性受限于评估方法本身的偏差，旨在揭示反馈的真实潜力并纠正评估缺陷。

Method: 构建合成数据（带明确真实答案）与自然数据，对比有无反馈条件下模型修订的效果；通过人工与自动评估结合，分析反馈对问题修复的影响，并识别评估偏差来源。

Result: 反馈驱动的模型修订在解决目标问题上显著优于基线；但在评估中，即使模型已正确修复问题，LLM裁判仍常误判并偏好原始基线输出，暴露了评估系统的系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 用户反馈是高度可操作的学习信号，其实际效用被现有评估方式严重低估；改进评估机制是释放反馈潜力的关键。

Abstract: Harnessing naturally occurring feedback from user interactions offers a promising learning signal for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies suggest this feedback is inherently noisy and difficult to leverage effectively. We challenge this conception by demonstrating that user feedback is a highly actionable signal for improvement, and that its perceived ineffectiveness stems from a systematic bias in current evaluation paradigms. To isolate the usefulness of feedback, we construct synthetic data with a definitive ground truth, alongside naturalistic data to validate that our findings hold in real-world scenarios. By comparing model revisions generated with and without access to feedback across both settings, we show that feedback-informed revisions resolve targeted issues at significantly higher rates than baseline revisions. Finally, we expose the root of the evaluation bias: when a model successfully fixes an issue exclusively due to feedback, LLM judges frequently fail to identify the genuinely corrected response, systematically preferring inferior baseline outputs instead.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [WMLLM: Self-Evolving Optimization Agents via Predict-Then-Act World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01608)
*Zhongzheng Li,Qingsong Ran,Shikun Feng,Nian Ran,Wenhao Li,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yue Wang,Xiaoguang Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测-行动世界建模的自进化优化代理框架WMLLM，通过利用大语言模型的隐含知识预测有前景的优化方向，结合多轮精炼、基于种群的搜索和强化学习，显著提升了黑箱优化任务中的样本效率和最终性能。在多目标分子优化基准测试中，WMLLM在有限评估预算下达到了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑箱优化方法因依赖直接候选生成或试错精炼，常面临样本效率低的问题。为提升搜索效率，需引入世界建模以提前识别有潜力的优化方向。大语言模型具备预测候选结果的能力，可作为有效工具支持高效优化。

Method: 提出WMLLM框架，采用预测-行动策略：先利用大语言模型预测潜在优化方向，再生成候选解；结合多轮代理精炼、种群搜索与强化学习，实现对世界模型和优化策略的持续迭代优化。

Result: 在多个黑箱优化任务中，尤其是多目标分子优化场景下，WMLLM展现出更高的样本效率和更优的最终性能，在有限评估预算下达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: WMLLM通过融合大语言模型的预测能力与自进化优化机制，为解决高维、弱结构化的黑箱优化问题提供了高效且可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Black-box optimization problems remain challenging because of large, weakly structured, and high-dimensional search spaces. Existing methods often suffer from poor sample efficiency because they rely on direct candidate generation or trial-and-error refinement. A natural way to improve search efficiency is to use world modeling, which can help identify promising optimization directions before costly evaluation. Large language models can predict the outcomes of these candidates with nontrivial accuracy because of their implicit knowledge. Motivated by this observation, we propose WMLLM, a self-evolving optimization-agent framework based on predict-then-act world modeling. The agent first predicts promising directions and then acts to generate candidates. Combined with agentic multi-turn refinement, population-based search, and reinforcement learning, WMLLM refines both its implicit world model and its optimization strategy during search. Experiments on black-box optimization tasks, especially multi-objective molecular optimization, show that WMLLM improves sample efficiency and final optimization performance. On the multi-objective molecular optimization benchmark, WMLLM achieves state-of-the-art results under a limited evaluation budget.

</details>


### [181] [DiDrive: A Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion Framework for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01609)
*Qisong Guo,Jingtang Chen,Zhilin Chen,Pei Xu,Mingjian Fu,Wenxi Liu,Yuanlong Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiDrive提出了一种基于分布引导的离线扩散框架，结合风险感知分层扩散（RHDif）和3DICE策略优化，有效缓解了分布偏移、重尾风险信号、分布外（OOD）动作生成及高维状态冗余等问题，在CARLA基准测试中表现优异，尤其在复杂交通场景下实现85%成功率和4295.68平均奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习策略在自动驾驶中易受分布偏移、重尾风险、分布外动作生成及高维状态冗余的影响，导致决策不安全、不稳定。

Method: 采用风险感知分层扩散（RHDif）架构处理状态空间中的冗余与安全威胁，通过低层风险门控编码器和高层上下文调制器聚焦关键威胁；在动作空间引入3DICE策略优化，利用样本内校准引导、时空优化和集成候选排序，抑制分布外过估计与梯度振荡。

Result: 在CARLA基准测试中，DiDrive显著优于IQL、CQL和Diffusion-QL等基线方法，尤其在包含60辆车辆的高密度交通场景中，成功率达85%，平均奖励达4295.68，验证了其在复杂环境下的鲁棒性与安全性。

Conclusion: DiDrive通过分布引导的双组件设计，为自动驾驶中的安全决策提供了高效且可靠的解决方案，具备在真实复杂交通环境中部署的潜力。

Abstract: While diffusion models effectively capture multimodal behavioral priors for autonomous driving, offline reinforcement learning (RL) policies remain susceptible to distribution shift, heavy-tailed risk signals, out-of-distribution (OOD) action generation, and high-dimensional state redundancy. To address these challenges, we propose DiDrive, a distribution-guided offline diffusion framework featuring two synergistic components: the Risk-Aware Hierarchical Diffusion (RHDif) architecture and the 3DICE policy optimization paradigm. In the state space, RHDif utilizes a low-level risk-gated encoder and a high-level contextual modulator to filter environmental redundancy and focus on safety-critical threats. In the action space, 3DICE mitigates OOD overestimation and gradient oscillation through in-sample calibrated guidance, spatiotemporal optimization, and ensemble-based candidate ranking. Evaluations on the CARLA benchmark demonstrate DiDrive's superiority over baselines like IQL, CQL, and Diffusion-QL, particularly in complex, high-density traffic scenarios with 60 vehicles, where it achieves an 85% success rate and a 4295.68 average reward, providing a robust pathway for safe autonomous driving decision-making.

</details>


### [182] [Prompt-Space Meta-Learning Does Not Transfer Across Users: A Frozen-LLM Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01615)
*Liam Byrne,David Dylan,Orla Fitzgerald,Eoin Doyle,Ciara Nolan,Padraig Lynch,Sinead Gallagher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出Muse方法，通过反射式提示演化在元训练用户群体上优化单一共享适应提示，并零样本应用于未见用户。但在两个标准个性化基准测试中，Muse的表现并未显著优于其原始种子提示或结构破坏控制组，在评分任务上甚至被简单的少样本检索方法超越（Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001）。研究归因于‘元目标坍缩’机制：元验证目标对用户-支持对应关系的真实性不敏感（LaMP-2: p=0.555；LaMP-3: p=0.622），导致无法学习可迁移的跨用户适应，反而奖励指令打磨和验证过拟合。研究提出了一套可复用的对照协议，以区分真正学到的适应与混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化冻结大语言模型常被建模为提示空间中的元学习问题，但缺乏对优化的元目标是否真正编码了跨用户可迁移适应能力的检验，而非仅提升通用指令质量。

Method: 提出Muse方法，通过反射式提示演化在元训练用户群体上优化共享适应提示，冻结后零样本应用于新用户；并设计多种控制组（种子提示、错误支持、不变性虚拟控制）来隔离干扰因素。

Result: Muse在两个基准测试中均未显著优于基线，且在评分任务上被少样本检索方法显著超越（Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001）；元验证目标对用户-支持真实性不敏感（p>0.5），表明存在元目标坍缩。

Conclusion: 现有基于元学习的提示个性化方法可能因元目标坍缩而无法学习真正可迁移的用户适应策略，应通过严格控制实验分离真实适应与指令优化等混淆因素。

Abstract: Personalizing a frozen large language model (LLM) to individual users is often framed as a meta-learning problem in prompt space: each user is a task, and one seeks a shared natural-language adaptation policy that, given a handful of the user's labeled interactions, configures the frozen model for that user. The framing is attractive because it is backbone-agnostic and reuses the machinery of prompt optimization, yet the field rarely tests whether the optimized meta-objective encodes transferable cross-user adaptation rather than generic instruction quality. We study this question with Muse (Meta-learned User-adaptation via Shared Evolution), which evolves a single shared adaptation prompt over a meta-train user population by reflective prompt evolution, freezes it, and applies it zero-shot to held-out users; matched controls isolate learning from confounds of phrasing and selection. On two standard personalization benchmarks (LaMP-2 categorization and LaMP-3 rating) over 200 held-out users each, Muse does not significantly improve on its own un-evolved seed prompt or on a structure-broken control that meta-trains on mismatched user-support pairs, and is dominated by plain few-shot retrieval on the rating task (Delta MAE +0.175, p < 0.001). We attribute these outcomes to a single mechanism, meta-objective collapse: the meta-validation objective is statistically invariant to whether the user-support correspondence is genuine (p=0.555 on LaMP-2, p=0.622 on LaMP-3), so it cannot be optimized into transferable adaptation and instead rewards instruction polish and validation overfitting. The seed-prompt, wrong-support, and invariance-oracle controls form a reusable protocol that separates learned adaptation from these confounds.

</details>


### [183] [Efficient Context-Limited Telescope Bibliography Classification for the WASP-2025 Shared Task Using SciBERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01647)
*Madhusudhana Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SciBERT的自动化方法，用于对科学论文进行四类分类（科学、仪器、提及、非望远镜），在512个标记的严格限制下实现了0.89的宏F1分数，表现优于WASP-2025排行榜上的其他方法。研究分析了文本截断的影响，表明即使一半样本超过长度限制，由于领域对齐，SciBERT仍能保持稳健分类性能，并探讨了截断、分块与长上下文模型之间的权衡，为科学文本整理提供了效率优化思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前望远镜文献目录的创建主要依赖人工，耗时且资源密集。自动分类科学论文以识别与特定望远镜相关的研究，有助于评估天文台的科学影响并保障可重复性，因此亟需高效自动化方法。

Method: 采用SciBERT模型进行文本分类，针对最大512词元的上下文长度限制，通过优化输入处理策略，在不显著牺牲性能的前提下实现高效分类。实验对比了不同处理方式如截断、分块和长上下文模型的优劣。

Result: 在严格上下文长度约束下，该方法取得0.89的宏F1分数，位居WASP-2025竞赛榜首，证明了其在有限计算资源下的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 尽管存在长度限制，基于领域预训练的SciBERT在科学文本分类任务中表现出色，结合合理的文本处理策略，可在保证精度的同时显著提升文献管理效率，为天文数据治理提供可行的技术路径。

Abstract: The creation of telescope bibliographies is a crucial part of assessing the scientific impact of observatories and ensuring reproducibility in astronomy. This task involves identifying, categorizing, and linking scientific publications that reference or use specific telescopes. However, this process remains largely manual and resource intensive. In this work, we present an efficient SciBERT-based approach for automatic classification of scientific papers into four categories - science, instrumentation, mention, and not telescope. Despite strict context-length constraints (maximum 512 tokens) and limited compute resources, our approach achieved a macro F1 score of 0.89, ranking at the top of the WASP-2025 leaderboard. We analyze the effect of truncation and show that even with half the samples exceeding the token limit, SciBERT's domain alignment enables robust classification. We discuss trade-offs between truncation, chunking, and long-context models, providing insights into the efficiency frontier for scientific text curation.

</details>


### [184] [CliffRank: A Dual-Branch Framework for Activity-Cliff Ranking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01673)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Peiyu Yang,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CliffRank提出了一种结合绝对活性回归与排序一致性学习的新方法，通过均方误差、阈值列表损失和成对偏好一致性（PPC）提升活性悬崖排名性能。在多个抗菌肽和小分子数据集上表现优异，尤其在小分子数据集上实现了0.6890的平均Spearman相关系数，且召回率表现良好。但其性能受PPC策略影响，需进一步优化调度机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有活性悬崖排名面临局部结构变化导致显著活性差异，且高质量数据有限的问题。为更有效利用已有活性标签，需改进模型对相对排序的一致性建模能力。

Method: CliffRank采用双并行预测器，结合均方误差损失、阈值列表损失和成对偏好一致性（PPC）损失，使预测结果在偏好-概率空间中保持相对排序一致性。

Result: 在三个抗菌肽数据集上，CliffRank+ESM2-t12达到最高平均Spearman相关系数0.5393和平均Recall@50 21.4；在三个小分子数据集上，CliffRank+PNA（PPC延迟至120轮后启用）实现最高平均Spearman相关系数0.6890，召回率30.4与ACANet-PNA持平。

Conclusion: CliffRank在多种数据集上表现出色，证明了排序一致性学习的有效性，但其性能受限于PPC策略的设计。未来应扩展评估范围，开发自适应PPC调度，并引入蛋白质或膜环境信息以提升模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Activity-cliff ranking remains difficult because local structural changes can cause large activity differences, while high-quality data that resolve the underlying mechanisms remain limited. To use available activity labels more effectively, we combine absolute-activity regression with ranking-consistency learning. CliffRank trains two parallel predictors with mean squared error, a thresholded listwise loss, and Pairwise Preference Consistency (PPC), which aligns relative ordering in the preference-probability space. On three antimicrobial peptide datasets, CliffRank with ESM2-t12 achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.5393 and mean Recall@50 of 21.4, although the leading method varied across individual datasets. On three small-molecule datasets, CliffRank with PNA, where PPC was activated after 120 epochs, achieved the highest mean Spearman correlation of 0.6890, while its mean Recall@50 of 30.4 matched that of ACANet-PNA. The PPC results also define its practical limits. Asymmetric initialization improved the MolCLR-GIN averages but did not improve every target. For PNA without pretrained weights, delayed PPC improved selected metrics, but no schedule was best for both mean Spearman correlation and mean Recall@50. Future work should evaluate more targets and antimicrobial peptide systems, develop adaptive PPC schedules, and incorporate protein or membrane context when available.

</details>


### [185] [Sim2Signal: Sim-to-Real Benchmarks for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01676)
*Ferdous Al Rafi,Susrik Mukherjee,Latika Liladhar Dekate,Jennifer Yawa Lavoe,Huaiyuan Yao,Shlok Mohanty,Longchao Da,Xuesong Zhou,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sim2Signal 基准，系统分解并评估交通信号控制中的模拟到现实（Sim-to-Real）差距，涵盖观测、动作、转移和奖励四个维度的失配。通过在33种不同设置和10个基于真实地点的网络上测试18种缓解方法，发现直接迁移性能普遍下降，且降级严重程度无法预测缓解效果；有效方法多依赖于对差距的估计，而非域随机化或不变表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习在交通信号控制中虽在仿真中表现良好，但部署至真实世界时普遍存在Sim-to-Real差距，现有缓解方法的效果缺乏系统评估，且领域内缺少统一基准来量化该差距。

Method: 构建 Sim2Signal 基准，将 Sim-to-Real 差距分解为观测、动作、转移和奖励四类失配，并在统一协议下分别引入每类差距；在多个真实网络与多种配置下评估18种缓解方法的表现。

Result: 直接迁移在所有差距源下均导致性能下降；缓解方法的有效性高度依赖于具体网络与差距设置；最有效的策略是估计差距本身，而非使策略对差异不敏感。

Conclusion: Sim-to-Real差距的来源复杂，单一缓解策略难以普适；未来方法应聚焦于对差距的建模与估计，而非依赖域随机化等泛化手段。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning achieves strong traffic signal control performance in simulation, yet policies trained in simulators often fail once deployed in the real world, a failure known as the Sim-to-Real gap. When RL is applied to traffic signal control, this gap arises from several sources: sensing, action execution, traffic dynamics, and the control objective. Their relative impact and the reliability of existing Sim-to-Real mitigation methods remain insufficiently understood, and the field lacks a standard benchmark for systematically measuring the gap and evaluating mitigation methods. We present Sim2Signal, a benchmark that decomposes the Sim-to-Real gap into observation, action, transition, and reward gaps, corresponding to mismatches in the four components of the underlying MDP, and induces each gap in isolation under a shared protocol. We evaluate 18 mitigation methods on 2 base controllers, across 33 gap settings and 10 calibrated networks built from 5 real-world locations. We find that direct transfer consistently degrades performance across all four gap sources, but the severity of the degradation does not predict the effectiveness of mitigation. Instead, mitigation effectiveness depends strongly on the network and gap setting: outside the action gap, a method that helps in one case may fail in another. The most effective methods generally estimate what the gap changes, rather than make the policy insensitive through domain randomization or invariant representations. Our code is available at https://github.com/Red-Pheonix/Sim2RealTSCBenchMark

</details>


### [186] [A Survey on Self-Improving Test-Time Intelligence: Feedback-Driven Adapting, Learning, and Scaling at Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01679)
*Shuaicheng Niu,Guohao Chen,Yaofo Chen,Zhiquan Wen,Jinwu Hu,Zeshuai Deng,Deyu Chen,Shuhai Zhang,Renjie Chen,Zihao Lian,Shoukai Xu,Gang Dai,Yunbei Zhang,Wei Luo,Yifan Zhang,Mingkui Tan,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一视角——反馈驱动的测试时智能（Test-Time Intelligence, TTI），用于理解AI系统在部署阶段通过测试时信息和额外计算自我改进的能力。该框架整合了测试时适应、测试时学习和测试时扩展三种方法，揭示了它们之间的差异与日益融合的趋势，为视觉、语言、多模态学习、生成模型、机器人学和医疗等领域的自适应AI系统研究提供了概念基础和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在部署过程中需要具备动态优化行为的能力，而现有研究分散于不同方向（如测试时状态调整、利用额外推理资源），术语和方法各异，缺乏统一视角，阻碍了跨领域知识融合与进步。

Method: 提出反馈驱动的测试时智能（TTI）作为统一框架，系统梳理测试时适应、测试时学习与测试时扩展三类方法，并分析其关联与融合趋势，结合多个应用领域进行综述。

Result: 建立了连接不同测试时改进方法的统一认知体系，明确了各类方法的核心机制与交叉点，为未来研究提供了清晰的路线图和理论基础。

Conclusion: 测试时智能是实现自适应AI系统的关键路径，本综述通过统一框架促进了跨领域理解，推动了对部署阶段自我改进机制的深入研究。

Abstract: The ability of AI systems to improve their behavior during deployment is becoming increasingly important. As inference moves beyond the static execution of a fixed trained model, a growing body of work studies how models can refine their behavior on the fly by exploiting test-time information and additional computation. These developments have largely evolved along two directions: methods that modify the model's state using test-time signals, and methods that improve predictions through extra inference-time resources such as more sampling and tool use. However, these directions are often studied in separate communities with different terminology, making their connections harder to see. In this survey, we present feedback-driven Test-Time Intelligence (TTI) as a unified perspective for understanding such deployment-time improvement. We use this view to relate test-time adaptation, test-time learning, and test-time scaling, highlighting both their distinctions and their growing overlap in hybrid systems. This unified framework helps connect previously fragmented ideas and provides a clearer conceptual foundation for studying inference-time self-improvement. We review major methodological paradigms, representative applications, and open challenges across vision, language, multimodal learning, generative models, robotics, and healthcare. Our goal is to provide a coherent foundation and research roadmap for the study of self-improving AI systems at test time.

</details>


### [187] [Reinforcement Learning and Rule-Based Peer-to-Peer Pricing in Residential PV-BES Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01680)
*Pablo Benalcazar,Maciej Kalka,Wilian Guamán,Jacek Kamiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文比较了基于规则和基于学习的定价机制在住宅光伏社区点对点电力交易中的表现。基于规则的基准方法包括账单分摊、中间市场价和供需比定价；基于学习的方法采用深度Q网络（DQN），评估了基于乘数和可学习的SDR形状定价，以及固定参数的SDR变体作为非学习控制。通过社区节省成本及财务与运营指标综合评估性能。在仅光伏的基础配置下，基于规则的方法优于最佳强化学习策略；加入储能后，最佳强化学习策略的社区节省从734.23欧元提升至978.52欧元。在所有学习型模式中，基于SDR形状的定价优于基于乘数的参数化方式。结果表明，当两类方法直接比较时，基于规则的定价依然具有很强竞争力，储能显著提升了学习型方法的表现，但收益分配在各家庭间仍不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索在住宅光伏社区中，基于规则与基于学习的定价机制在点对点电力交易中的有效性，以提升社区整体能源经济性，并分析储能系统对不同定价策略的影响。

Method: 采用深度Q网络（DQN）实现强化学习定价机制，对比多种规则基定价方法（如账单分摊、中值市场价、供需比定价），并测试不同参数化形式（乘数型与可学习的SDR形状）的性能，结合社区总节省、财务与运营指标进行评估。

Result: 在仅光伏配置下，规则基方法优于最优强化学习策略；引入储能后，强化学习策略的社区节省显著提升（从734.23欧元增至978.52欧元）；可学习的SDR形状定价优于乘数型参数化；尽管学习方法在储能支持下表现更优，但收益分配不均。

Conclusion: 基于规则的定价机制在当前条件下仍具高度竞争力，储能系统的引入能显著提升学习型定价策略的效益，但需关注收益分配的公平性问题。

Abstract: This paper compares rule-based and learning-based pricing mechanisms for peer-to-peer (P2P) electricity trading in residential photovoltaic communities. The rule-based benchmarks comprise bill-sharing as an ex post allocation mechanism, the mid-market rate, and supply-demand-ratio pricing. The reinforcement-learning (RL) formulation is implemented through a Deep Q-Network and evaluated under multiplier-based and learnable SDR-shaped pricing, with a fixed-parameter SDR variant as a non-learning control. Performance is assessed through community savings together with complementary financial and operational indicators. In the base PV-only configuration, the rule-based benchmarks outperform the best RL policy. With battery energy storage, evaluated for the RL policies only, community savings under the best RL policy increase from EUR 734.23 to EUR 978.52. Across the learning-based modes and in both configurations, SDR-shaped pricing outperforms the multiplier-based parameterization considered. The results indicate that rule-based pricing remains highly competitive wherever the two families are compared directly, and that storage substantially improves the learning-based outcomes under this accounting, while the distribution of benefits remains heterogeneous across households.

</details>


### [188] [Median-of-Means as an Extremal Convex Estimator and a Nonconvex Route to the Trimmed Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01689)
*Angshul Majumdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从确定性优化视角重新审视了中位数均值估计，提出了一类用于处理重尾和对抗性污染数据的块-Lp 估计器。在至少有1-ε比例良好块的块污染模型下，证明了所有凸块M估计器的最坏情况鲁棒性常数至少为1/(1-2ε)，这与经典中位数均值界一致，并表明在凸类中无法达到1/(1-ε)的修剪块最优常数。随后引入非凸块-Lp族（p∈(0,1)），推导出所有全局最小值的有限样本确定性鲁棒性界；随着p从1减小到0，这些界逐渐逼近最优常数。当p足够小时，全局最小值与理想情况下的解一致。此外，块-Lp目标函数具有良性景观，所有局部极小值都接近真实值且无不良盆地。结合块级集中不等式，得到在有限2+δ阶矩下的次高斯偏差界，并拓展至高维鲁棒均值估计和稀疏回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决重尾分布和对抗性污染数据下的鲁棒学习问题，突破传统凸估计器的鲁棒性上限，实现更优的鲁棒性能。

Method: 基于确定性优化视角，构建非凸块-Lp估计器家族，分析其鲁棒性界、优化景观及高维扩展性质。

Result: 证明了凸估计器的鲁棒性下界，提出了非凸块-Lp估计器，实现了逼近最优鲁棒性的有限样本界，且全局最小值与理想解一致，优化景观优良，适用于高维鲁棒统计学习。

Conclusion: 非凸块-Lp估计器在重尾和对抗性污染数据下具有优异的鲁棒性和统计性能，可实现接近理论最优的鲁棒性，并具备良好的优化特性，适用于高维鲁棒学习任务。

Abstract: We revisit median-of-means estimation from a deterministic optimization viewpoint and develop a family of block-Lp estimators for robust learning with heavy-tailed and adversarially corrupted data. In a block contamination model with at least a fraction 1 minus epsilon of good blocks, we first show that every convex block M-estimator has worst-case robustness constant at least 1 divided by 1 minus 2 epsilon. This matches the classical median-of-means bound and proves that the trimmed-block oracle constant 1 divided by 1 minus epsilon cannot be attained within the convex class. We then introduce a nonconvex block-Lp family for p between 0 and 1 and derive finite-sample deterministic robustness bounds for all global minimizers. As p decreases from 1 toward 0, these bounds continuously approach the trimmed-block oracle constant. For sufficiently small p, the global minimizers coincide with those of the oracle under a mild separation condition. We also show that the block-Lp objectives have a benign landscape, with all local minima remaining close to the truth and no bad basins. Combining these results with block-level concentration yields sub-Gaussian deviation bounds under finite 2 plus delta moments and high-dimensional extensions to robust mean estimation and sparse regression.

</details>


### [189] [Tri-Band Channel Measurement-Enabled Multi-Layer Digital Twin for Terahertz Wireless Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01699)
*Mingjie Zhu,Ziming Yu,Guangjian Wang,Chong Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于测量驱动的多层数字孪生框架，用于太赫兹无线数据中心，涵盖物理、信道、评估和操控层。通过140/220/300 GHz的实测数据构建校准后的物理孪生，并利用隐式神经场建立AI信道孪生，实现高效信道重建与实时预测。实验表明该框架在低误差下完成实时推理，且天花板部署的接入点可实现90%以上覆盖率（SINR > 10 dB），验证了其在无线规划与优化中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能计算快速发展，数据中心对灵活、高容量的互连需求激增。太赫兹通信具备超大带宽和高空间复用能力，是未来无线数据中心的潜在解决方案；而数字孪生技术可实现无线网络的高效规划与实时优化，因此亟需一种融合测量与建模的数字孪生框架以支持太赫兹无线数据中心的设计与部署。

Method: 提出多层数字孪生框架：首先在140/220/300 GHz进行大规模信道测量，获取频率相关的传播特性；基于测量数据，联合优化几何、材料、天线及混合传播模型，构建测量校准的物理孪生；在此基础上，引入面向视距（LoS）的隐式神经场，学习位置相关的信道统计特性，构建AI信道孪生，实现接收功率与视距概率的实时预测；进一步构建系统级评估层，分析接入点到机架及机架间通信的覆盖与干扰性能。

Result: 所提AI信道孪生在信道功率重建上优于现有神经场基线方法，同时保持实时推理能力；天花板部署的接入点在10 dB SINR阈值下实现超过90%的覆盖范围，验证了该数字孪生框架在太赫兹无线数据中心规划与优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本工作提出的测量驱动多层数字孪生框架有效融合了实测数据与智能建模，实现了从物理层到系统层的渐进式构建，为太赫兹无线数据中心的高效规划、实时优化与性能评估提供了可靠工具，具有重要的应用前景。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI computing has driven increasing demands for flexible and high-capacity data-center interconnections. Owing to its ultra-wide bandwidth and high spatial reuse capability, terahertz (THz) communication has emerged as a promising solution for future wireless data centers, while digital twins (DTs) enable efficient wireless planning and real-time optimization. In this work, a measurement-driven multi-layer DT framework is proposed for THz wireless data centers, where the physical, channel, evaluation, and manipulation layers are progressively constructed from bottom to top. First, extensive channel measurements are conducted at 140, 220, and 300 GHz to characterize frequency-dependent propagation behaviors. Based on the tri-band measurements, a measurement-calibrated physical twin is established by jointly optimizing the geometry, material, antenna, and hybrid propagation models. On top of the physical twin, a line-of-sight (LoS)-aware implicit neural field is developed to construct an AI channel twin for efficient channel reconstruction. The proposed AI twin learns location-dependent channel statistics from the calibrated twin, enabling real-time prediction of received power and LoS probability. Building upon the reconstructed channel field, a system-level evaluation layer is derived to analyze coverage and interference for both AP-to-rack and rack-to-rack communications. Experimental results show that the proposed AI twin achieves lower power reconstruction error than existing neural-field baselines while maintaining real-time inference capability. Moreover, the ceiling-mounted AP deployment achieves over 90% coverage under a 10 dB signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) threshold, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed DT framework for THz wireless data-center planning and optimization.

</details>


### [190] [Generative Diffusion Surrogates with Analytical Variance Schedule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01705)
*Patrick Reichherzer,Gianluca Gregori,David N. Hosking,Subir Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出将生成扩散模型中的噪声调度与物理系统的均方位移（方差）时间导数相匹配，从而将生成时间校准为运输过程的物理时间。通过强制构建方差路径，模型能够准确模拟从弹道到扩散的湍流等离子体中粒子输运的非高斯分布演化，无需中间时刻的物理数据或超参数调优，实现了可校准的代理建模和基于似然的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生成扩散模型的噪声调度通常凭经验设定，缺乏物理意义；而在输运问题中，均方位移（方差）往往可通过宏观理论或经验标度获得，因此可以利用这一信息来建立具有物理意义的时间尺度，提升模型在复杂输运系统中的准确性与可解释性。

Method: 将扩散模型的前向噪声过程设计为与已知的方差随时间变化路径一致，即噪声率等于方差的时间导数。模型通过学习反向得分场来恢复初始结构，同时保持方差路径受控，从而实现对非高斯分布演化的精准捕捉。

Result: 在弹道到扩散的湍流等离子体输运场景中，该方法成功拟合了测试粒子分布，准确再现了实验室测量的方差尺度，并跟踪了模拟的峰度演化，且无需调整噪声调度，表现出良好的泛化能力和物理一致性。

Conclusion: 通过将生成扩散模型的时间轴与物理输运过程的方差演化对齐，该方法实现了具有物理意义的、可校准的随机输运代理模型，为复杂系统提供了高效且可信的模拟与推断工具。

Abstract: Stochastic transport describes physical systems in which an initially structured distribution spreads under unresolved forcing, scattering, or heterogeneous media. Useful surrogates for such systems should be probabilistic, time-resolved, and able to represent non-Gaussian distributional structure. Generative diffusion models, which corrupt data with Gaussian noise and learn a reverse flow back to structured states, have these properties. Their noise schedules, however, are usually chosen heuristically: image and audio generation---the canonical use cases---provide no physical clock. In transport, by contrast, the variance, or mean-square displacement, is often known from macroscopic theory or empirical scaling even when the full distribution is not. Here we prescribe the forward noising rate as the time derivative of this variance, turning generative time into a calibrated transport clock. The variance path is enforced by construction, while the learned score field represents how non-Gaussian structure inherited from entrance data is smoothed along that path, requiring no intermediate-time physical transport data. For ballistic-to-diffusive transport in turbulent plasmas, the surrogate matches test-particle distributions, reproduces the laboratory-measured variance scale, and tracks the simulated kurtosis evolution without schedule tuning, enabling calibrated emulation and likelihood-based inference.

</details>


### [191] [RecKAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with a Learnable Recursive Polynomial Basis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01729)
*Amirhosein Azarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecKAN introduces a learnable polynomial basis via a second-order recurrence relation, enabling dynamic adaptation beyond fixed classical bases like B-splines or Chebyshev polynomials. It outperforms existing KAN variants across multiple benchmarks in classification and forecasting, shows superior fitting on oscillatory functions, and demonstrates interpretable learned recurrence coefficients that shift from classical polynomial structures.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing KANs use fixed function bases (e.g., Chebyshev, splines), limiting flexibility. The motivation is to allow the basis itself to be learned, enhancing model adaptability and performance.

Method: RecKAN employs a learnable second-order polynomial recurrence $R_{n+1}(x) = (ax^2+bx+c)R_n(x) + (dx+e)R_{n-1}(x)$, where all five coefficients are trained jointly with the network. This recurrence generalizes classical polynomial families and enables variable degree growth.

Result: RecKAN surpasses parameter-matched KAN baselines on all classification tasks and achieves the lowest MSE on ETTh1 forecasting. It improves accuracy over MLP heads on image datasets and accurately fits sharply oscillatory functions. Learned recurrence coefficients show interpretable shifts away from classical linear-degree regimes.

Conclusion: By learning the recurrence basis, RecKAN achieves greater flexibility and performance than fixed-basis KANs, with theoretical and empirical evidence supporting its adaptability and interpretability.

Abstract: Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed scalar weights of a standard network with learnable univariate functions on each edge, but existing variants still fix the \emph{basis} that those functions are built from: B-splines, Chebyshev polynomials, wavelets, or Jacobi polynomials, and learn only the combination weights over it. We introduce RecKAN, which instead defines the basis itself by a second order polynomial recurrence, $R_{n+1}(x) = (ax^2+bx+c)R_n(x) + (dx+e)R_{n-1}(x)$, whose five coefficients are learned jointly with the network. We show this recurrence recovers several classical polynomial families including both kinds of Chebyshev polynomials, Fibonacci, Pell, and Jacobsthal polynomials as special cases, and prove that its degree grows linearly in $n$ exactly on the sub-family containing all of them, giving a concrete sense in which the learned basis can move beyond any fixed classical choice. Across multiple benchmark datasets spanning image, text, biomedical time series classification, and time series forecasting, RecKAN outperforms three parameter-matched KAN baselines (Chebyshev, Jacobi, and spline based) on all classification tasks and achieves the lowest MSE on the ETTh1 forecasting benchmark. Additionally, when used as a classifier head with a convolutional backbone, RecKAN achieves higher accuracy than standard MLP heads on Fashion MNIST, CIFAR-10, and SVHN. On a synthetic function fitting benchmark it tracks a sharply oscillatory target that a parameter comparable MLP under fits. We further show that the learned recurrence coefficients are interpretable: on the task requiring the most local structure, training moves the basis away from the linear degree growth regime that contains every classical family we identify, consistent with our theoretical analysis of what that structural shift enables.

</details>


### [192] [CAT-Flow: Curvature-Adaptive sTeps for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01746)
*Qinchan Li,Pedro Cisneros-Velarde,Keru Fu,Samuel Antunes Miranda,Sharan Vaswani,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种轻量级、无需训练的算法CAT-OV和CAT-OT，通过建立流匹配采样与梯度流之间的新联系，在推理时自适应调整步长，显著提升生成效率。该方法无需额外神经网络评估，利用有限差分近似时间导数（CAT-OT）或向量场梯度（CAT-OV）估计曲率，具有常数阶截断误差，实验证明在四个文本到图像流匹配模型上优于现有启发式步长策略，生成质量相当的情况下可减少高达40%的生成步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型虽性能优异，但其基于ODE的迭代采样过程存在效率瓶颈，生成质量高度依赖步长选择，通常需20-30步才能获得高质量样本，亟需更高效的自适应步长策略以减少计算开销。

Method: 提出CAT-OV与CAT-OT两种推理时自适应步长算法：CAT-OT通过有限差分近似向量场的时间导数来估计时间维度上的曲率；CAT-OV则通过向量场的梯度近似状态空间中的曲率，二者均不引入额外神经网络计算，且理论分析表明具有常数阶截断误差。

Result: 在四个文本到图像流匹配模型上，CAT-OV与CAT-OT在图像质量指标上均优于现有步长启发式方法，可在保持相同生成质量的前提下将所需步数减少最多达40%，显著提升采样效率。

Conclusion: 本工作揭示了流匹配采样与梯度流之间的深层联系，并提出了高效、无需训练的自适应步长机制，为提升流匹配模型的生成效率提供了有效解决方案，具备广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Flow Matching has emerged as a leading framework for generative modeling, powering state-of-the-art systems such as FLUX and Stable Diffusion 3.5. However, the iterative nature of its ODE-based sampling process creates a fundamental efficiency bottleneck: the quality of generated samples is highly sensitive to the choice of step-sizes, and current models typically require 20 to 30 steps for good quality. In this work, we propose two lightweight, training-free algorithms, CAT-OV and CAT-OT that adapt step-sizes at inference time based on a novel connection between Flow Matching sampling and gradient flow. Our algorithms are computed efficiently by not requiring additional neural function evaluations. Specifically, CAT-OT estimates curvature over time via a finite-difference approximation of the time-derivative of the vector field, while CAT-OV approximates curvature over the state space via a gradient of the vector field. Under suitable conditions, both methods have truncation error bounds of constant order. Empirically, CAT-OV and CAT-OT outperform existing step-size heuristics in image quality metrics across four text- to-image Flow Matching models, reducing the number of generation steps required to reach comparable quality by up to 40%.

</details>


### [193] [A Study of Conditional Diffusion Models for Open-Loop Control under Dry Friction and Stiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01756)
*Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Action Diffusion, a diffusion-based method for generating action sequences, outperforms random shooting and CEM in controlling point-mass systems with dry friction and stiction, especially with limited samples. It generates temporally coherent controls by leveraging structured priors.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address the challenge of generating effective control sequences in systems with stiction and dry friction, where only actions above a static-friction threshold initiate motion, requiring temporally structured and sparse control inputs.

Method: A conditional 1D U-Net is used to generate bounded control sequences based on initial and target states, forming a diffusion-based open-loop policy. The model learns from a structured dataset of valid control primitives.

Result: Action Diffusion achieves lower terminal error and fewer stuck steps compared to random shooting, prior-guided shooting, and CEM, particularly in low-sample settings.

Conclusion: Conditional diffusion models effectively generate temporally coherent control sequences that overcome stiction by recombining learned control primitives, making them suitable for state-to-state open-loop control.

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as expressive generative priors for planning and control. This paper studies Action Diffusion, an action-sequence diffusion formulation used as an open-loop proposal distribution for a point-mass system with dry friction and stiction. In this benchmark, motion starts only when the applied input exceeds a static-friction threshold, so effective controls occupy a small and temporally structured subset of the action-sequence space. A compact conditional 1D U-Net generates bounded control sequences conditioned on initial and target states. We compare it with uniform random shooting, random shooting from the same structured dataset prior, and the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). Results show that Action Diffusion reduces terminal error and stuck steps, especially in low-sample regimes. These results indicate that conditional diffusion provides an effective mechanism for generating temporally coherent control sequences that overcome stiction by conditioning and recombining structured control primitives from the training prior for state-to-state open-loop control.

</details>


### [194] [Toward Explainable and Policy-Aware AI for Carbon Credit Price Prediction: A Research Framework for Emerging Carbon Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01765)
*Summaiya Unnisa Begum,Mohammed Nadeem Ullah,Mohammed Abdul Ghani Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出EPA-CarbonNet，一种六层架构，融合市场数据与政策文本，通过交叉注意力和校准区间实现可解释的碳信用价格预测。在11年日度碳指数数据上测试，结果显示模型表现不佳：随机游走优于模型在五日均方根误差（0.0365 vs 0.0475），SHAP排名相关性仅0.54，政策注意力未与已知监管事件重合；但方向准确性（58.6%）优于所有基线。代码、数据文档及全部结果均可在GitHub获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有碳市场价格预测研究集中于欧盟和中国碳市场，将政策文本压缩为情感分数，缺乏校准与解释稳定性，且难以准确预测价格变化。亟需更可解释、政策感知强的模型以提升预测可靠性。

Method: 提出EPA-CarbonNet，采用六层架构，结合市场序列与政策文本，利用交叉注意力机制进行特征融合，并引入校准区间与政策归因解释方法，增强模型可解释性与政策敏感性。

Result: 实验表明模型整体表现不佳：随机游走优于模型在五日均方根误差（0.0365对0.0475），SHAP重要性排序在重采样下相关性仅为rho=0.54，政策注意力未与实际监管事件对齐；但方向预测准确率达58.6%，高于所有基线。

Conclusion: 尽管提出的EPA-CarbonNet在可解释性和政策感知方面具有设计优势，但在实际预测性能上未能超越简单基准，表明当前融合政策文本与市场数据的方法仍面临挑战，未来需改进模型对政策信号的捕捉能力。

Abstract: Carbon markets put a price on emissions, yet that price remains hard to forecast. Work in this area clusters on the EU and Chinese schemes, compresses regulatory text into a sentiment score, and reports accuracy without calibration or explanation stability. We distil ten recurring gaps into an impact-feasibility matrix and propose EPA-CarbonNet, a six-layer architecture that fuses market series with policy text by cross-attention and calibrated intervals alongside policy-attributed explanations. We then build and test it on eleven years of daily S and P carbon index data. The findings are largely negative, and reported as measured: a random walk beats the model on five-day RMSE (0.0365 against 0.0475), SHAP rankings agree at rho = 0.54 across resampled backgrounds, and policy attention never coincides with documented regulatory events. Directional accuracy, at 58.6 percent, leads every baseline. Code, data documentation and all result artifacts are available at https://github.com/Kimalice/Toward-Explainable-and-Policy-Aware-AI-for-Carbon-Credit-Price-Prediction

</details>


### [195] [Emergence of Fibrations, Compression, and Symmetry Breaking in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01768)
*Osvaldo M Velarde,Lucas C Parra,Alireza Hashemi,Hernan A Makse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了深度神经网络学习过程中生成图论中的局部对称性（纤维化和覆盖），证明这些对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子。实验证明，多种主流网络架构均出现此类对称性，利用这些对称性可将模型压缩至原大小的17%而不损失性能；通过受控破坏对称性，还可提升持续学习中的可塑性，达到当前最优表现。理论成果为基于对称性的可解释AI系统提供了新基础，使黑箱变为可解释的彩色图，并实现更高效的推理与终身学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络被视为难以理解的黑箱，缺乏可解释性和高效性，亟需从理论层面揭示其内在结构机制，以提升透明度、压缩效率和持续学习能力。

Method: 通过数学证明覆盖对称性是随机梯度下降的稳定吸引子；在多类神经网络架构中检测并验证对称性存在；设计基于对称性压缩与可控破坏的算法，实现模型压缩与持续学习优化。

Result: 成功在多种网络中观测到覆盖对称性；模型可压缩至原大小的17%而性能不变；通过破坏对称性显著提升持续学习表现，达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络的学习过程自然产生稳定的覆盖对称性，这一发现为构建可解释、高效且具备终身学习能力的AI系统提供了全新理论框架，推动从黑箱向可解释彩色图的范式转变。

Abstract: Artificial neural networks are often regarded as powerful yet opaque black boxes. Here, we demonstrate that learning in deep neural networks generates local symmetries known in graph theory as fibrations and coverings. We prove that covering symmetries are stable attractors of stochastic gradient descent. Consistent with this theory, we report the emergence of covering symmetries across major network architectures, including multilayer, convolutional, recurrent, and transformer networks. Exploiting these symmetries enables drastic model compression - reducing networks to 17% of their original size without sacrificing performance. Furthermore, controlled breaking of covering symmetry overcomes the loss of plasticity, achieving state-of-the-art performance in continual learning. The theoretical results provide a new foundation for AI systems based on symmetries that convert black boxes into interpretable colored graphs and enable more efficient inference and lifelong learning.

</details>


### [196] [D-FROST: Decentralized Federated pRompt-tuning via Optimal tranSporT for Non-IID and Imbalanced Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01802)
*Quan Minh Nguyen,Hoang M. Ngo,Trong Nghia Hoang,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次研究了在去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中使用提示调优（prompt tuning）的问题。针对本地数据异构性导致的提示集索引不一致问题，提出了一种基于最优传输（OT）的去中心化提示调优算法D-FROST，通过运输匹配将邻近客户端的提示合并为紧凑的代表性提示集。同时，建立了关于网络级提示中位数收敛到稳定点附近以及Wasserstein共识误差的理论边界，实验表明D-FROST在异构客户端数据下有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化联邦学习中，全模型更新成本过高，而提示调优因其参数高效性成为理想选择；然而，由于本地数据异构性导致提示集无法对齐，传统平均方法失效，亟需一种能保证共识与收敛性的新算法。

Method: 将去中心化提示调优建模为基于Wasserstein距离的优化问题，提出D-FROST算法，利用最优传输机制实现邻居提示集之间的匹配与融合，并通过理论分析证明其收敛性与共识误差上界。

Result: D-FROST在异构数据环境下实现了有效的去中心化提示调优，能够达成客户端间的一致性，且网络级提示中位数收敛至接近驻点，实验验证了其优越性能。

Conclusion: 本工作首次系统研究了去中心化联邦学习中的提示调优，提出的D-FROST算法通过最优传输机制解决了提示集不对齐问题，具备理论保障和实际有效性，为高效、可扩展的联邦学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Prompt tuning provides a parameter-efficient way to adapt foundation models (FMs) by freezing the pretrained backbone and updating only a small set of learnable prompts. This property makes prompt tuning especially suitable for decentralized federated learning (DFL), where exchanging full-model updates can be prohibitively expensive. However, prompt tuning in DFL introduces new challenges. Prompt sets learned from heterogeneous local data may not be index-wise aligned, making standard decentralized averaging unsuitable. In addition, the algorithm should be theoretically guaranteed to achieve consensus and make progress toward the shared objective. In this work, we provide the first study of prompt tuning in DFL. We formulate decentralized prompt tuning as a Wasserstein-based optimization problem over prompt measures, which captures the set-valued structure of prompts. We then propose D-FROST, an optimal-transport-based (OT-based) decentralized prompt-tuning algorithm that merges neighborhood prompts into compact representative prompt sets through transportation-based matching. We further analyze D-FROST by bounding the Wasserstein consensus error across clients, and establishing convergence of the network-level prompt barycenter to a neighborhood of stationarity. Experiments under heterogeneous client data demonstrate the effectiveness of D-FROST for decentralized prompt tuning.

</details>


### [197] [hLLM: Single Pass Decoding for Generative Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01807)
*Emil Laftchiev,Prachi Agrawal,Moe Kayali,Bixing Yan,Qi Xu,Zijie Lei,Chen Qiu,Zhi Hua,Ke Li,Luke Simon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出hLLM，一种针对排序任务的高效解码方法，通过匈牙利算法在O(1)次前向传播中完成N个序号的生成，显著提升推理速度（64倍加速），同时保持与教师模型相当的排名质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型在生成排序结果时采用自回归解码，效率低下，而排序任务只需输出特定的序号排列，因此可设计专用解码策略以提高效率。

Method: 利用轻量级自注意力头从预填充隐藏状态中读取N×K的项目-位置得分矩阵，再通过匈牙利算法求解最优二分图匹配，直接生成有效排列，无需后续修复。结合LoRA微调与教师排序蒸馏进行训练优化。

Result: 实现了28毫秒的端到端推理时间，相比传统方法提速64倍，且排名质量与教师模型持平；系统性消融实验验证了架构、训练信号和骨干网络适配的贡献。

Conclusion: hLLM将生成式排序与组合优化相结合，为实时排序提供了新的O(1)解码路径，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art generative ranking quality, but the ranking they produce must be decoded, and autoregressive decoding spends one sequential forward pass per emitted token. We observe that the only tokens a ranker must emit are the $N$ ordinal values naming the items in ranked order, and that this narrow, permutation-structured output format admits decoding strategies which are much more efficient than left-to-right generation. We introduce hLLM (Hungarian LLM), a format-specialized decoding strategy that decodes all $N$ ordinals in $O(1)$ forward passes. hLLM reads an $N \times K$ item-position score matrix off the LLM's prefill hidden states with a lightweight self-attention head, then decodes the ordinals as the optimal bipartite assignment of that matrix via the Hungarian algorithm, yielding a valid permutation by construction rather than by repair. Through a systematic study of training signals and backbone adaptation, we show that LoRA-based fine-tuning combined with teacher ranking distillation reaches 28 ms end-to-end inference, a speed-up of $64\times$ while maintaining ranking quality on par with the teacher. We provide a complete ablation decomposing the contributions of architecture, training signal, and backbone adaptation. Our framework connects generative ranking to combinatorial optimization, opening a path toward other $O(1)$-decode mechanisms for real-time ranking.

</details>


### [198] [Import What You Need: Learning When and How to Augment EHR Graphs with External Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01839)
*Chen Chen,Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Dongjie Wang,Mei Liu,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReTA提出一种基于强化学习的动态拓扑增强框架，通过在每次就诊时根据患者状态自适应选择知识图谱（KG）导入策略（软导入、硬导入或跳过），以应对电子健康记录（EHR）中数据稀疏和不规则的问题。该方法结合离线构建的KG模板池与分通道编码器，实现语义与结构信号的解耦处理，并通过自适应门控融合，提升模型性能与可解释性。在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上的实验表明，ReTA在诊断预测、死亡率和再入院等任务上均优于现有基线，具备良好的泛化能力与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对知识图谱的引入是静态且上下文无关的，无法适应患者状态的动态变化，导致在稀疏和不规则的电子健康记录中表现受限。因此需要一种能够根据患者当前状态动态决定是否以及如何引入外部知识的机制。

Method: ReTA采用强化学习框架，将知识图谱的引入视为每就诊一次的预算感知决策过程。它从三个动作中选择：软导入（仅丰富节点特征）、硬导入（嫁接紧凑的KG子图以建立消息传递捷径）和跳过（当基础编码器已足够自信时保持不变）。为稳定训练，使用解耦编码器分别处理语义和结构信息，并通过自适应门控融合。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，ReTA在诊断预测、死亡率和再入院任务中均显著优于多个强基线模型；具有良好的跨数据集和跨知识图谱的迁移能力；生成的增强模式可解释性强；在低监督条件下仍表现出稳健提升，证明了动态知识引入的优势。

Conclusion: ReTA通过动态、上下文感知的知识图谱增强机制，在稀疏医疗数据中实现了更高效、更准确的纵向预测，同时兼顾模型效率与可解释性，为EHR建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Longitudinal prediction from electronic health records (EHRs) is limited by the sparsity and irregularity in patient trajectories, and knowledge augmentation with external knowledge graphs (KGs) offers a promising way to alleviate these issues. However, most existing methods perform fixed, context-agnostic topology augmentation by adding the same KG nodes and edges regardless of a patient's evolving state. We propose ReTA, a Reinforcement learning-based dynamic Topology Augmentation framework that casts KG import as a per-visit, budget-aware policy. ReTA first constructs an offline refined pool of KG-grounded templates, then learns a policy to select one augment action per visit from three options: Soft Import, which enriches node features without modifying graph topology, Hard Import, which grafts a compact KG subgraph onto the visit graph to create message-passing shortcuts, and Skip, which leaves the visit unaugmented when the base encoder is already confident. To stabilize learning, ReTA employs a decoupled encoder that processes semantic and structural signals in separate channels and fuses them via adaptive gating. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV across diagnosis prediction, mortality, and readmission show that ReTA consistently outperforms strong baselines while remaining efficient, transfers across datasets and knowledge graphs, and yields interpretable augmentation patterns. The robust gains under sparse supervision highlight the advantage of ReTA's dynamic decision to import knowledge, boosting accuracy while curbing costs.

</details>


### [199] [OutageDiT: A Generative Foundation Model for Power Outage Forecasting and Scenario Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01896)
*Yunqin Zhu,Feng Qiu,Yao Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OutageDiT 是一种用于生成美国七天停电轨迹的基底模型，以每15分钟的分辨率进行预测，利用全美范围内的停电和天气数据训练。该模型通过条件编码器处理历史背景和未来已知协变量，并使用浅层流解码器生成完整的停电轨迹，支持点预测、不确定性量化和条件事件模拟。在多个停电预测基准测试中，OutageDiT 在预测准确性和场景质量上优于现有强基线模型，并能实现零样本迁移至未包含区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严重事件罕见，单一地区数据中极端停电与恢复模式样本极少，难以有效建模不确定性及时间依赖性，因此需要一种能够生成高质量停电情景的通用方法。

Method: 采用条件编码器处理历史上下文与未来协变量，结合浅层流解码器复用时序状态，生成高分辨率（每15分钟）的七天停电轨迹，实现端到端的生成式建模。

Result: OutageDiT 在多个停电预测基准上显著提升预测精度与场景质量，具备零样本迁移能力，可支持点预测、不确定性量化与条件事件模拟，为基于不确定性的运营规划提供新路径。

Conclusion: OutageDiT 成功将停电预测转化为可操作的条件仿真工具，为电力系统在不确定性下的运行规划提供了强大支持。

Abstract: Power-outage planning requires scenarios before an event occurs. These scenarios must represent uncertainty in magnitude, timing, and duration while preserving temporal dependence. However, severe events are rare, and data from any single region contain few examples of extreme outage and restoration patterns. To address this challenge, we introduce OutageDiT, a foundation model for generating seven-day outage trajectories at quarter-hour resolution, trained on outage and weather records across the United States. Specifically, a condition encoder processes the historical context and known future covariates once per forecast, and a shallow flow decoder reuses the resulting horizon-aligned states to generate complete trajectories. The resulting samples support point forecasting, uncertainty quantification, and conditional event simulation within one deep generative model. Across outage forecasting benchmarks, OutageDiT improves forecast accuracy and scenario quality over strong baselines and supports zero-shot transfer to held-out regions. Together, these results position conditional outage simulation as a bridge from outage forecasting to operational planning under uncertainty.

</details>


### [200] [CRISP: Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling with Structural-Mass-Motivated Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01925)
*Huu Huy Nguyen,Chien Van Nguyen,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Linh Ngo Van,Jieyang Chen,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRISP提出一种输入自适应的稀疏预填充方法，解决长上下文大模型推理中自注意力计算瓶颈问题。通过直接读取代理注意力图的结构特征替代传统JSD路由，消除计算开销；并引入基于噪声底限的汇点感知阈值，有效抑制长序列下的累积背景噪声，实现高达5.30倍的注意力加速，且在检索任务上性能超越基线最多达28.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏注意力方法依赖固定模式或离线分析，缺乏输入自适应能力；现有动态方法虽能实时路由，但依赖间接代理且忽略后softmax质量分布层次，导致效率与精度受限。

Method: 提出C_struct结构代理，直接从注意力图中提取关键位置质量信息，替代JSD路由；设计基于噪声底限的sink-aware阈值机制，避免长序列中累积背景噪声影响，实现高效且精准的动态稀疏化。

Result: 在InfiniteBench、RULER和LongBench多个基准上表现最优，检索任务性能提升最高达28.0个百分点，512k token下达到5.30倍注意力加速，同时保持结构完整性。

Conclusion: CRISP通过结构感知路由与噪声敏感阈值设计，显著提升了动态稀疏注意力在长序列推理中的效率与准确性，为大规模长上下文模型优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: The attention prefilling phase of long-context LLM inference scales quadratically, making self-attention a severe computational bottleneck. Traditional sparse attention methods mitigate this through fixed patterns or offline profiling, but lack the flexibility to adapt to input-dependent attention structure. Recent dynamic methods address this by routing heads to sparse patterns in real-time, but rely on indirect routing proxies with overhead and budget allocation mechanisms that overlook the post-softmax mass hierarchy. We present CRISP (Cliff-awaRe Input-adaptive Sparse Prefilling), which identifies and addresses two structural challenges in this dynamic routing paradigm. First, we show that the routing decision can be read directly off the structure of the proxy attention map.
