We replace the Jensen-Shannon Divergence (JSD) routing with C_struct, a structural proxy that measures mass at Vertical-Slash compatible positions and reproduces JSD's routing decisions while eliminating both the pooled matmul and subsequent KL divergence overhead. Second, we formalize the post-softmax mass cliff and demonstrate theoretically that strictly cumulative coverage thresholds accumulate O(n) background noise at long contexts. CRISP navigates this via a sink-aware threshold grounded in the noise floor. Empirically, across InfiniteBench, RULER and LongBench on two model families, CRISP is the strongest sparse method overall and matches or exceeds exact dense attention on retrieval-heavy benchmarks, recovering up to +28.0 pp on retrieval tasks over baselines and achieving up to a 5.30x attention speedup at 512k tokens, driven primarily by our O(n) noise elimination during selection while preserving structural integrity.

</details>


### [201] [OR-Transformer: Scaling Real-Time Decision-Making to 1,000 Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01933)
*Shuze Daniel Liu,David Simchi-Levi,Claire Chen,Chutong Gao,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OR-Transformer 是一种基于深度强化学习的联合补货框架，采用物品排列等变的 Transformer 架构和路径梯度训练方法，可高效处理大规模、高维、随机需求下的供应链补货问题。在高达 1,024 个库存项的场景中，其性能随规模增长超越学习基线与滚动时域 MILP 方法，并将在线决策时间缩短超过 400 万倍，实现实时大规模强化学习应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统滚动时域随机混合整数线性规划（MILP）在大规模供应链补货问题中计算效率过低，而标准强化学习方法在高维动作空间中面临信用分配难题，亟需一种可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出 OR-Transformer 框架，利用物品排列等变的 Transformer 结构建模多物品间的依赖关系，结合路径梯度法直接优化库存动态中的长期收益，实现端到端的联合补货策略学习。

Result: 在最多 1,024 个库存项的问题中，OR-Transformer 随规模增大表现持续优于基线方法；在线决策速度比 MILP 求解器快超过 400 万倍，支持实时大规模强化学习决策。

Conclusion: OR-Transformer 成功解决了大规模供应链联合补货中的可扩展性与实时性挑战，为复杂库存管理提供了高效、可扩展的深度强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: Modern supply chain operations can require coordinating replenishment across thousands of heterogeneous items under correlated stochastic demand, heterogeneous lead times, and shared fixed ordering costs, yielding observation spaces exceeding $10^4$ dimensions. At this scale, rolling-horizon stochastic mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) become prohibitively slow, while standard reinforcement learning (RL) methods face increasingly challenging credit assignment in high-dimensional action spaces. We introduce OR-Transformer, a deep reinforcement learning framework for joint replenishment under stochastic demand, with an item-permutation-equivariant Transformer architecture and pathwise-gradient training through the inventory dynamics. Across problem sizes up to 1,024 inventory items, OR-Transformer increasingly outperforms learning-based and rolling-horizon MILP baselines as scale grows. It also reduces online decision-making time by over 4 million times relative to MILP solvers, enabling real-time, large-scale deep RL in supply chain operations.

</details>


### [202] [Refining Heuristic-Based Bitcoin Address Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01942)
*Hugo Schnoering,Roman Bresson,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络对比嵌入的比特币地址聚类优化方法，旨在解决现有启发式方法在用户级活动分析中因地址归属不准确导致的聚类错误问题。通过释放公开的比特币交易图数据集、设计与启发式规则一致的地址嵌入学习方法，并结合层次聚类实现更精细的分析和可疑合并的量化检测，显著提升了聚类质量与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币的伪匿名性使得单个用户可能控制多个地址，现有启发式方法在地址归属判断上存在聚类扁平化、模块性差及误合并不同用户的问题，影响用户行为分析的准确性。

Method: 利用图神经网络生成对比嵌入，将启发式聚类结果作为先验知识进行嵌入学习；结合层次聚类对原始聚类进行细化，并引入定量标准识别潜在错误合并。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能有效提升聚类精度，发现并标记出原启发式方法中可疑的跨用户合并，同时提供可解释的细粒度用户行为分析能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于对比嵌入的聚类优化框架能够显著改善比特币地址聚类的质量，为区块链用户行为分析提供了更可靠、可扩展的技术支持。

Abstract: Bitcoin's pseudonymous nature makes it challenging to analyze user-level activity, since a single user may control multiple identifiers (addresses). Existing heuristic-based methods attempt to identify addresses belonging to the same user, but they often produce flat cluster assignments with limited modularity and are prone to errors such as merging different users together. In this work, we propose a method for refining heuristic-obtained clusters by grounding our clustering on contrastive embeddings yielded by graph neural networks. Our contributions are threefold: (i) we release a publicly available dataset of Bitcoin transaction graphs containing a substantial number of clusters; (ii) we propose a methodology for learning address embeddings consistent with heuristics, and back it up with theoretical guiding intuitions; (iii) through hierarchical clustering, we enable a finer analysis of heuristic clusters and provide a quantitative criterion for flagging suspicious merges.

</details>


### [203] [On-Policy Distillation Meets Off-Policy GRPO: Training Compact Instruction-Following Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01947)
*Vignesh Prabhakar,Jialing Pan,Anil Babu Ankisettipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段框架，结合离策略教师优化与在线策略学生蒸馏，通过强化学习提升紧凑型指令跟随重排序器性能。在MAIR-11和MAIR-Full数据集上均超越传统蒸馏方法，尤其在分布外场景表现优异，且在效率与质量间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒸馏方法受限于教师输出的固定排名空间，无法充分捕捉多样化的排序信号；为突破此限制，引入强化学习视角，增强学生对教师知识的动态吸收能力。

Method: 第一阶段使用4B教师模型通过离策略GRPO算法结合LLM判别反馈进行优化；第二阶段让1B学生模型基于自身策略采样排名，并接收由教师生成的软奖励信号，实现探索与知识迁移的耦合。

Result: 在MAIR-11上达到0.7670 nDCG@6，比离线列表级蒸馏高4.6点；在MAIR-Full上取得最高任务宏平均得分（0.6808 nDCG@6, 0.7865 MRR@6）；优于两个7B RL训练的重排序器，并在不同架构的学生模型中保持一致提升。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的在线策略蒸馏能有效提升紧凑型重排序器在分布外场景下的泛化能力，同时实现高质量与高效率的平衡，为轻量化指令跟随重排序器部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Compact instruction-following rerankers are attractive for deployment, but conventional distillation pipelines typically train students by offline imitation of teacher outputs on a fixed set of examples, constraining supervision to the teacher's observed ranking space. We revisit reranker distillation through the lens of reinforcement learning.
  We propose a two-stage framework combining off-policy teacher optimization with on-policy student distillation. In Stage 1, a 4B teacher reranker is strengthened with off-policy GRPO using LLM-judge feedback on 88K instruction-following examples. In Stage 2, a compact 1B student samples rankings from its own policy and receives soft teacher-derived rewards on those rankings, coupling student exploration with knowledge transfer.
  Our strongest gains appear under distribution shift. On MAIR-11, the original 11-subset, 869-query evaluation, the proposed student reaches 0.7670 nDCG@6, outperforming offline listwise KD by +4.6 points. Controlled comparisons against offline pairwise RankNet KD and on-policy GKD show that neither changing the offline distillation objective nor moving teacher-distribution matching on-policy reproduces the performance of reward-based on-policy distillation over student-sampled rankings. The advantage persists on MAIR-Full: across all 126 tasks and 9,356 queries, the proposed method obtains the highest task-macro point estimates among the evaluated distillation variants, reaching 0.6808 nDCG@6 and 0.7865 MRR@6. It also exceeds two released 7B RL-trained rerankers on the comparable MAIR-11 evaluation, while the same Stage 2 training procedure consistently improves three architecturally distinct alternative student backbones.
  On the 9,861-query validation benchmark, the resulting 1B reranker achieves 0.7624 nDCG@6 while providing a favorable quality-efficiency tradeoff relative to larger alternatives.

</details>


### [204] [Convergence Theory of Knowledge Distillation in Asynchronous P2P Gossip Learning Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01952)
*Lucas Qingyang Fang,Tiyao Liu,Jinhao Jing,Zeji Li,Kaijie Chen,Harikrishna Kuttivelil,Katia Obraczka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在去中心化、异步对等网络中使用知识蒸馏（KD）的收敛性理论，通过将共识从参数空间转移到函数空间，证明了在标准平滑性和方差假设下，时间平均的函数平稳性和函数空间分歧以 $O(1/(ηT))$ 的速率收敛到一个由 $O(η)+O(B_f^2)+O(ζ_f^2)$ 定义的邻域。实验表明，与孤立训练相比，KD 在多种网络结构中将函数分歧减少了 40-61 倍，并验证了预测的瞬态行为和步长权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的分布式随机梯度下降（decentralized SGD）在设备架构不同、模型参数数量不一致的情况下无法定义，因为无法对参数进行平均。知识蒸馏（KD）通过交换软标签而非权重，绕过了这一障碍，但目前缺乏对完全去中心化、异步对等网络中 KD 的收敛性理论支持。因此，本文旨在建立这样的理论框架。

Method: 论文将共识从参数空间转移到函数空间，将每次 KD 事件视为 logit 空间中预测分布上的几何收缩算子，并在基于参考测度的预测希尔伯特空间中进行分析。引入两个可实现性假设：一是将参数梯度下降映射到函数步骤，二是控制受限任务与知识蒸馏之间的对齐关系。通过数学推导，在标准假设下得到收敛速率。

Result: 理论结果表明，时间平均的函数平稳性和函数空间分歧以 $O(1/(ηT))$ 的速率收敛至 $O(η)+O(B_f^2)+O(ζ_f^2)$ 邻域。实验结果显示，无论是在同构、宽度异构还是混合家族网络中，KD 均能将函数分歧减少 40-61 倍，而孤立训练无此效果；采样平稳性诊断显示晚期瞬态指数为 0.99–1.90，四点步长扫描也验证了预期的邻域-步长权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文首次建立了完全去中心化、异步对等网络中知识蒸馏的收敛性理论，通过函数空间的视角揭示了其稳定机制，实验证明 KD 显著降低函数分歧并具有可预测的动态特性，为异构设备间的协同学习提供了理论基础与实践指导。

Abstract: Decentralized, serverless learning increasingly connects devices running different architectures, where the standard tool, decentralized SGD, is undefined as models with different parameter counts cannot be averaged. Knowledge distillation (KD) exchanges soft predictions rather than weights and sidesteps this obstacle, yet convergence theory for fully decentralized, asynchronous peer-to-peer (P2P) KD is lacking. We provide one, relocating consensus from parameter space to function (output) space: a KD event is a geometric contraction operator in logit space on the peers' predictive distributions, which we analyse in the Hilbert space of predictions on a reference measure. Under standard smoothness/variance assumptions and two realizability assumptions, one bridging parameter SGD to the functional step and one controlling restricted task/KD alignment, the time-averaged functional stationarity and function-space disagreement converge at rate $O(1/(ηT))$ to an $O(η)+O(B_f^2)+O(ζ_f^2)$ neighbourhood. Here $B_f$ is the distance from the task optimum to the peers' reachable classes and $ζ_f$ measures persistent local-task heterogeneity. Across homogeneous, width-heterogeneous, and mixed-family networks of the experiments, KD contracts function disagreement by $40-61\times$, while isolated training does not. The sampled stationarity diagnostic has late transient exponents $0.99-1.90$ on the shared-skeleton main runs, and the four-point step-size sweep exhibits the predicted transient: neighbourhood tradeoff.

</details>


### [205] [FlashKAN: B-Spline KANs via Truncated Power Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01956)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashKAN accelerates KANs by replacing the slow Cox-de Boor recursion with a faster truncated power form, enabling a single GPU kernel via torch.compile, stabilizing training with input clamping, and providing an open-source drop-in replacement.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The standard Cox-de Boor recursion for B-spline activations in KANs is computationally expensive, consuming over 90% of forward-pass time, which limits scalability and efficiency.

Method: FlashKAN uses the truncated power form to express cubic B-splines as shifted (x)_+^3 terms, implements a fused GPU kernel via torch.compile, applies bounded-coordinate stabilization by clamping inputs to [0, k+1], and delivers a production-ready PyPI package.

Result: FlashKAN achieves significant speedup by eliminating recursion and memory-heavy operations, improves numerical stability, and enables efficient deployment as a drop-in replacement for KAN layers.

Conclusion: FlashKAN dramatically enhances the efficiency and practicality of KANs through algorithmic innovation and optimized implementation, paving the way for broader adoption in deep learning.

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) place learnable B-spline activations on network edges rather than fixed activations on nodes. The standard Cox-de Boor recursion evaluates these activations through k sequential passes for degree-k splines, consuming over 90% of forward-pass time. FlashKAN replaces this recursion with the truncated power form, a classical result from approximation theory that expresses each uniform cubic B-spline as five (x)_+^3 terms at shifted knot positions. This paper makes three contributions: (1) a torch.compile-fused implementation that collapses these operations into a single GPU kernel, eliminating all recursion, span lookup, and scatter-gather operations; (2) a bounded-coordinate stabilization that clamps the normalized input to [0, k+1], preventing the catastrophic cancellation that historically motivated the Cox-de Boor recursion; and (3) a production-ready, open-source package (pip install flashkan) that serves as a drop-in replacement for existing KAN layers.

</details>


### [206] [A Unified Particle Filter LSTM for Data-Driven Process Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01967)
*Parvin Malekzadeh,Opher Baron,Dmitry Krass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种统一粒子滤波LSTM（Unified PF-LSTM）模型，通过维护一组加权的递归状态假设来解决事件日志中过程状态不完整的问题，利用加权均值和基于矩生成函数的特征表示，实现对下一活动的概率预测与当前活动停留时间的条件分位数预测，该模型在三个真实急诊科数据集上表现优于现有数据驱动基线方法，尤其在动态复杂且观测不全的情况下优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统递归模型将每个过程前缀压缩为单一确定性状态，无法处理事件日志中因缺少服务开始时间而导致的潜在状态不确定性问题，而实际过程可能有多种合理解释，因此需要更灵活的状态表示机制。

Method: 提出一种基于粒子滤波思想的LSTM框架——统一粒子滤波LSTM（Unified PF-LSTM），通过维护一个加权的递归状态假设集合，并利用其加权均值及基于矩生成函数的特征提取方式，生成可预测下一流程活动与停留时间的综合表示，支持端到端训练。

Result: 在三个真实世界的急诊部门数据集上，所提方法在流程路由、持续时间以及系统级行为重现方面均优于现有数据驱动基线模型，特别是在过程动态复杂且日志信息不完整时表现出更强的鲁棒性和准确性。

Conclusion: Unified PF-LSTM通过引入粒子滤波思想增强状态表示能力，有效应对部分可观测过程中的不确定性，为数据驱动过程模拟提供了一种更准确、更鲁棒的解决方案，尤其适用于日志信息不完整的现实场景。

Abstract: Data-driven process simulation aims to generate realistic case trajectories from historical event logs without requiring an explicitly specified model of the underlying dynamics. Deep sequence models can capture complex temporal dependencies through next-activity probabilities and conditional time distributions. However, event logs provide only a partial view of the underlying process state, often recording activity completions without the corresponding service-start times. Consequently, the same observed process history may be consistent with multiple plausible latent process conditions, whereas standard recurrent models compress each process prefix into a single deterministic recurrent state. We propose a Unified Particle Filter LSTM (Unified PF-LSTM) that maintains and sequentially updates a weighted set of recurrent-state hypotheses. We summarize this particle belief using its weighted mean and learned features based on the moment-generating function. The resulting representation is used to predict a categorical distribution over the next activity and conditional quantiles of the current activity's sojourn time. The framework is trained end-to-end from event-log data and evaluated on three real-world emergency department datasets. The results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms the considered data-driven baselines in reproducing routing, duration, and system-level behavior across all datasets, with particularly strong gains in settings where complex process dynamics are only partially reflected in the available event logs.

</details>


### [207] [CAHR-Net: Condition-Adaptive Hysteresis Reconstruction for Compact and Interpretable Magnetic Core Loss Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01991)
*Chunye Gong,Cong Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAHR-Net，一种条件自适应的磁滞回线重建网络，通过在物理作用点注入工作条件（频率、温度、波形统计），实现从磁感应强度波形到磁场重建、回线面积积分到功率损耗估计的可解释链路。采用特征级线性调制注入条件信息，并配合AdamW优化器、余弦调度及分阶段损失目标进行大规模训练。在MagNet A-E材料测试中，仅用1874参数即达到6.89%平均p95相对误差，优于所有对比方法，且相比最强黑箱模型参数量减少约48倍；显著降低最困难材料D的误差（从16.40%降至14.87%）。消融与条件切片分析表明性能提升源于物理回线重建、结构化条件调制与匹配优化轨迹的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁芯损耗模型多将频率、温度、波形等条件简化为终端标量，通过经验公式或数据驱动方式拟合，缺乏中间磁滞回线表示，导致物理机制不透明且难以精确建模复杂条件下的回线变形。本文旨在构建一个保留物理可解释性的条件自适应建模框架，使操作条件直接作用于中间回线重建过程。

Method: 提出CAHR-Net，通过特征级线性调制将频率、温度和波形统计信息注入中间重建表示，保持从磁感应强度到磁场重建、回线面积积分、功率损耗估算的完整可解释路径。采用AdamW优化器、余弦退火调度以及分阶段的重建-损耗联合目标函数进行大规模训练，以激活调制路径的有效性。

Result: 在MagNet A-E材料协议上，CAHR-Net实现平均p95相对误差6.89%，优于所有对比方法，且参数量仅为最强黑箱模型的约1/48；对最难材料D的误差由16.40%降至14.87%；消融实验验证了条件调制与优化策略的关键作用。

Conclusion: CAHR-Net成功实现了条件自适应的物理可解释磁滞回线重建，通过在中间表示中引入条件调制并配合匹配优化策略，在极低参数量下显著提升磁芯损耗预测精度，为高精度、轻量化电磁建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Magnetic core loss originates in the hysteresis loop: the energy dissipated per excitation cycle equals the loop area, and frequency, temperature, and waveform shape set the loss by reshaping the loop geometry. Most existing models let these conditions act only on a terminal scalar - empirical equations fold them into fitted exponents, and data-driven predictors append them to encoded features - so no intermediate hysteresis representation remains for the conditions to reshape. This paper proposes CAHR-Net, a condition-adaptive hysteresis reconstruction network that injects the operating conditions where they physically act. It preserves the interpretable chain from flux density waveform to magnetic field reconstruction, loop-area integration, and power loss estimation, and uses feature-wise linear modulation to inject frequency, temperature, and waveform statistics into the intermediate reconstruction representation. A matched large-batch training protocol based on AdamW, cosine scheduling, and a staged reconstruction-to-power-loss objective is also reported, because the modulation pathway takes effect only within it. On the MagNet final A-E material protocol, CAHR-Net attains an average p95 relative error of 6.89% with only 1874 parameters, the lowest among all compared methods, together with a lower worst-material p95 than the strongest black-box solution at about 48x fewer parameters; it reduces the average p95 of the physical reconstruction backbone from 7.47% to 6.89% and the p95 of material D, the most difficult material, from 16.40% to 14.87%. Ablation and condition-slice analyses attribute the improvement to the coupling of physical loop reconstruction, structured condition modulation, and the matched optimization trajectory.

</details>


### [208] [Train What You Deploy: Closing the MLP Reachability Gap in Low-Rank Clone Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02006)
*Wenhui Chen,Zhifeng Li,Jie Zhou,Navan Preet Singh,Madalina Ciobanu,Chenghua Wang,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的模型压缩方法，通过训练与部署一致的权重，恢复因传统方法导致的未使用容量。基于LRC（Low-Rank Clone）初始化，提出Dense-LRC和CORE-LRC两种实现方式，在不改变部署形状、参数量和推理计算量的前提下，使训练过程覆盖整个部署矩阵，从而显著提升性能。在多个教师模型（Llama3.2-3B、Llama3.1-8B、Qwen2.5-3B）上，相比基准模型平均提升2.36~10.45分，尤其在宽教师模型上表现突出，达到原方案200亿训练标记的准确率仅需100亿标记（2倍效率）。半参数1.5B学生模型在约9万亿标记的教师下实现相近性能，而2.7B学生模型以不到1/900的压缩标记数超越Meta官方压缩结果。所有结果均为单种子运行，验证了性能提升主要源于可训练空间扩大而非额外参数或训练策略变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型压缩方法如低秩克隆（LRC）在训练时仅优化教师诱导的子空间，导致部署时大量权重自由度无法触及，造成资源浪费。为解决此问题，需让训练过程覆盖实际部署的完整权重结构，以释放被闲置的模型容量。

Method: 基于相同初始条件的LRC骨架，提出两种可合并的实现方式：Dense-LRC与CORE-LRC，均保持最终部署矩阵完整，但将训练目标扩展至整个部署矩阵，确保训练所学即部署所用。该方法无需增加参数量或推理开销。

Result: 在三个不同规模的教师模型上，新方法相较匹配预算的基线模型平均提升2.36~10.45分（Avg9），最大提升出现在最宽的教师模型（Qwen）上，仅用100亿标记即可达到原方案200亿标记的精度，实现2倍训练效率；1.5B学生模型接近9万亿标记教师的性能，2.7B学生模型以极低压缩数据量超越Meta官方压缩结果。所有结果均来自单次种子实验，表明性能提升归因于训练空间扩大而非其他因素。

Conclusion: 通过‘训练即部署’原则，本工作成功恢复了传统压缩方法中被忽略的模型容量，实现了更高效率与更强性能的模型压缩，且无需牺牲推理成本。该方法具有高度可扩展性与实用性，适用于大规模语言模型的高效蒸馏与部署。

Abstract: A compressed student has two shapes that need not agree: the weight it deploys at inference and the weight family its training can reach. We show that a state-of-the-art weight-inheritance distiller, Low-Rank Clone (LRC), deploys a full-width student MLP but ties training to a teacher-induced slice, leaving 62.5-81.4% of each deployed matrix's independent linear degrees of freedom unreachable-paid for at inference, never trainable. Our principle is one line: train what you deploy. From the identical LRC warm start, we make the training object the entire deployed matrix, with no change in deployed shape, deployed parameter count, or inference FLOPs, via two mergeable realizations (Dense-LRC and CORE-LRC) that both collapse to one deployed weight. This recovers stranded capacity: taking the stronger realization per teacher, +2.36/+2.71/+10.45 Avg9 over matched-budget plain-LRC baselines across three teachers (Llama3.2-3B, Llama3.1-8B, Qwen2.5-3B), with the largest gain on the widest teacher (Qwen), where it reaches the original recipe's approx. 20B-token accuracy at 10B tokens (2x token efficiency); there the strictly same-lineage arm still recovers +6.39, the fully controlled figure. Controls strongly support attributing the gain to the enlarged reachable set, rather than to added parameters or the recipe. From approx. 10B distillation tokens plus a short SFT, a half-parameter 1.5B student matches its approx. 9T-token teacher's 9-task macro-average, within evaluation noise and with a residual MMLU deficit, and a 2.7B student beats Meta's own official compression of Llama3.1-8B at ~900x fewer compression tokens (a token count under unmatched recipes, not a compute claim). All results are from single-seed runs on the LRC backbone.

</details>


### [209] [Source-Free Class Relearning: Diagnosing Forgetting in Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02018)
*Zahra Dehghani,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在无源（source-free）条件下，通过仅使用已遗忘模型的分类头更新来恢复被遗忘类别。提出了一种白盒源自由重学审计（SFRA）方法，利用合成探测集和模型引导的置信度过滤生成高置信保留样本与低置信边界邻近样本，并重新标记为遗忘类。基于理论分析，证明了单次梯度步可提升遗忘类的期望logit裕度。引入重学分数（RS）量化恢复能力与保留性能的平衡，实验表明多种遗忘方法存在显著源自由可恢复性，部分甚至优于重新训练参考模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法虽降低遗忘类准确率，但可能仍保留可恢复的表示结构；且已有恢复方法依赖真实样本、辅助数据或参考检查点，限制了评估能力。因此需要一种无需外部数据、仅基于已遗忘模型的可恢复性评估机制。

Method: 提出白盒源自由重学审计（SFRA），包括：1）理论分析建立充分对齐条件；2）生成合成嵌入并用模型置信度筛选高置信保留探针与低置信边界探针；3）将边界探针重标为遗忘类进行重学；4）采用高斯采样与Softmax置信度，默认方案外还测试其他分布与不确定性指标。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上，多个遗忘方法表现出显著的源自由可恢复性；部分方法的重学分数（RS）甚至超过匹配的重新训练参考模型，说明其表示结构仍高度可恢复。

Conclusion: 即使遗忘类准确率较低，许多当前遗忘方法仍保留可被源自由方式有效恢复的结构。本研究揭示了现有遗忘方法在表示层面的潜在漏洞，并提出了一个有效的评估框架（SFRA）与量化指标（RS），推动更可靠的隐私保护模型设计。

Abstract: Class unlearning aims to remove a model's ability to recognize designated forget classes while preserving performance on retain classes. However, low forget accuracy after unlearning does not necessarily mean the class structure has been erased. Approximate unlearning methods can alter classifier decision boundaries while leaving recoverable structure in the representation. Prior work has shown that forget classes can be recovered, but existing approaches require real forget or retain samples, auxiliary data, or reference checkpoints. We study class relearning in a strictly source-free setting, asking whether a forget class can be recovered through a classifier-head update using only the unlearned model. Our approach rests on a theoretical analysis establishing a sufficient alignment condition under which a single gradient step on a synthetic probe set increases the expected logit margin of the forget class. Building on this, we propose a white-box Source-Free Relearning Audit (SFRA), which generates candidate embeddings in representation space and uses model-guided confidence filtering to construct high-confidence retain probes and low-confidence boundary-adjacent probes that are relabelled as the forget class. Gaussian sampling and Softmax confidence are used by default, while ablations with alternative proposal distributions and uncertainty criteria show that recoverability is not specific to these choices. To quantify recoverability, we introduce the Relearning Score (RS), which jointly measures forget-class recovery and retain-accuracy preservation, and report class-matched $Δ$RS relative to a retrained reference. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and TinyImageNet with ResNet-18, ViT-B/16, and Swin-T show that several unlearning methods exhibit substantial source-free recoverability, and that for a subset of methods this recoverability exceeds the matched retrained reference.

</details>


### [210] [Act More, Decide Less: Skill-Guided Adaptive Action Chunking for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02042)
*Yanting Yang,Can Jin,Jinman Zhao,Jiahao Wu,Yang Zhou,Zhepeng Wang,Zhendong Wang,Mu Zhou,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPACE提出一种新方法，通过从成功轨迹中提取两层程序化技能来指导长序列动作的边界学习，从而解决大语言模型代理在长期交互任务中因无法学习动作块边界而导致的效率问题。该方法结合了在线与离线策略优化，并引入感知动作块的信用分配机制，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld上显著提升成功率（7.0%-31.3%），同时将平均决策轮次减少高达78.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ReAct风格的LLM代理在长期任务中因每轮仅执行一个基本动作而效率低下，尽管可频繁重规划，但大量时间浪费在常规动作序列上。直接让代理输出可变长度的动作块虽为自然改进方向，但标准强化学习训练易导致代理退化为单动作行为或过度承诺过长序列，根本原因在于缺乏对动作块边界的建模能力。

Method: SPACE通过从成功轨迹中诱导出两层程序化技能（子技能边界即为动作块边界监督信号），并将该时序结构通过混合在线/离线策略优化方式，结合感知动作块的信用分配机制，将其蒸馏为一个基本动作-动作块策略。

Result: 在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld基准测试中，SPACE相较于最强基线，成功率提升7.0%至31.3%，同时平均决策轮次减少最多达78.9%，显著提升了长期任务中的效率与性能。

Conclusion: SPACE通过显式建模动作块边界并利用程序化技能引导训练，有效解决了长序列动作生成中的边界学习难题，为提升大语言模型代理在复杂、长期交互任务中的表现提供了高效且稳健的新范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents for long-horizon interactive tasks typically follow a ReAct-style protocol, issuing one primitive action per LLM round. While this enables frequent replanning, it is inefficient for long-horizon tasks where many rounds are spent on routine action sequences. A natural alternative is to let the agent emit variable-length action chunks. However, naively training such policies with standard reinforcement learning fails: the agent either collapses to single-action behavior or over-commits to excessively long sequences. Both failures share a common root cause: the inability to learn chunk boundaries. We propose SPACE, which addresses this challenge by distilling chunk-boundary supervision from trajectory-induced programmatic skills. We induce two-level programmatic skills from successful trajectories, where subskill boundaries serve as direct chunk-boundary supervision. This temporal structure is then distilled into a primitive-chunk policy via hybrid on-/off-policy optimization with chunk-aware credit assignment. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that SPACE improves success rates by 7.0%-31.3% over the strongest baseline in each setting while reducing average LLM decision rounds by up to 78.9%.

</details>


### [211] [The Dynamics of Continuous Mixture Collapse in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02049)
*Ali Backour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型（LLMs）在潜在状态推理中为何无法有效保留连续混合状态的问题。通过理论分析与实验验证，发现存在三个独立的失败原因：1）Transformer架构本身会扭曲混合状态的几何结构，训练过程进一步加剧这一问题；2）即使状态传输是线性的，softmax读出和自回归反馈构成的动力系统也会导致混合状态被放大或收缩，使各成分失衡或不可区分；3）当混合成分增多时，精确保持混合状态需要上下文相关的校正，其维度可能随成分数量增加而增长。实证结果表明，预训练模型的运行轨迹大多位于放大部分，且过渡点与理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索为何预训练语言模型在使用连续混合状态进行推理时难以保持多种可能的推理路径，从而揭示其潜在状态推理能力的局限性。

Method: 结合理论分析与受控的实证研究，对多种模型进行系统性分析，探究混合状态在模型中的演化行为，并通过数学建模预测动力系统的行为边界，再通过实验验证理论预测。

Result: 识别出三个独立的失败机制：架构固有的几何扭曲、由softmax与自回归反馈形成的动力系统导致的放大或收缩效应，以及多成分混合下所需校正维度随成分数增长的趋势。实验验证了理论阈值的存在，且大多数预训练模型处于状态放大的区域。

Conclusion: 预训练语言模型在保持连续混合状态方面存在根本性挑战，主要源于架构设计与训练动态的共同作用。要实现有效的潜在状态推理，需引入上下文依赖的校正机制，且其复杂度随推理路径多样性增加而上升。

Abstract: LLMs latent-state reasoning methods replace discrete intermediate tokens with continuous states, such as weighted mixtures of token embeddings, to retain multiple possible reasoning directions rather than committing to one. Yet pretrained language models often fail to preserve these mixtures. We study why through a combination of theoretical analysis and controlled empirical investigations on a variety of models. We identify three independent, distinct sources of failure. First, transformer architectures already distort mixture geometry, and training substantially amplifies this effect. Moreover, the failure can occur even if the model transports mixtures perfectly linearly: the softmax readout and autoregressive feedback form a dynamical system that either amplifies small differences until one component of the mixture dominates or contracts different mixtures until they become indistinguishable. We verify this theoretical prediction empirically: the observed transition between contraction and amplification occurs near the theoretical threshold derived by our analysis, and pretrained-model rollouts lie predominantly on the amplifying side. Finally, we generalize to mixtures of many components and show that exact preservation generally requires context-dependent correction, whose required dimensionality can grow with the number of components.

</details>


### [212] [DynG-Diff: A State-Aware Dynamic Guidance Diffusion Framework for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02068)
*Zhente Zhang,Zhengwei Ni,Wei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DynG-Diff框架，通过两阶段训练和轻量级状态感知策略网络，实现对多变量时间序列的动态引导扩散建模，有效应对变量间噪声水平与演化模式差异大的问题，提升在严重观测污染下的鲁棒性与预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法依赖任务特定的条件范式，缺乏灵活性，难以处理多变量时间序列中不同变量间存在的显著噪声水平和演化模式差异（即信息异质性）问题。

Method: 采用两阶段分离训练策略，使用无条件扩散主干网络建模多变量时间序列的联合分布；引入轻量级状态感知策略网络，实时推断变量可靠性并生成动态引导强度矩阵；将动态权重数学形式化为观测分布的局部精度，从而在推理时对高置信度变量进行精确引导，同时过滤异常噪声干扰。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在概率预测性能上优于最先进的条件扩散模型，并在严重观测污染下展现出更强的鲁棒性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: DynG-Diff通过动态引导机制有效缓解了多变量时间序列中因信息异质性带来的挑战，为复杂动态系统的概率预测提供了更灵活、鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic multivariate time series (MTS) forecasting is crucial for modeling complex dynamical systems. However, existing diffusion-based methods rely on task-specific conditional paradigms that lack flexibility and struggle with inherent "information heterogeneity"--the significantly varying noise levels and evolutionary patterns across variables. To address this, we propose DynG-Diff, a variable-sensitive dynamic guidance diffusion framework for probabilistic multivariate time-series forecasting: (1) DynG-Diff adopts a two-stage separated training strategy and uses an unconditional diffusion backbone to model the joint distribution of multivariate time series. (2) DynG-Diff introduces a lightweight state-aware policy network that adaptively infers variable reliability from real-time noisy states and one-step denoising estimates, outputting a dynamic guidance strength matrix. (3) DynG-Diff mathematically formulates this dynamic weight as the local precision of the observation distribution, enabling precise guidance for high-confidence variables during inference while filtering out interference from anomalous noise. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate competitive probabilistic forecasting performance against state-of-the-art conditional diffusion models and improved robustness under severe observation corruption.The implementation code is available at: https://github.com/TT-20011031/DynG-Diff

</details>


### [213] [XMerge: Cross-Axis Selection and Reconstructive Layer Merging for LLM Depth Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02083)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: XMerge是一种后训练深度压缩方法，通过跨轴选择和局部边界重建，在不改变架构或引入额外推理参数的情况下，有效减少Transformer层并保持模型性能。在多个Llama和Qwen模型（0.5B-8B）上，其在极端层移除（k=4）时表现最优，尤其在CORE和MMLU任务上优于现有基线，且避免了显著的困惑度上升。其优势在统计上显著，且在零样本和上下文学习中均表现稳定，是唯一未出现崩溃的算子。局部重建贡献主要增益，而跨轴融合在选择不一致时有帮助。额外构建成本可通过数万次请求后的解码节省回收。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度压缩方法在移除Transformer层时会损失大量性能，且性能下降不可预测。需要一种无需标签、无需微调、不改变架构的高效压缩方法，以在保持服务架构的同时实现高质量的层移除。

Method: XMerge包含两个组件：1）跨轴选择（Cross-axis selection），通过低相对幅度和角度变化识别可移除的块；2）局部边界重建（Local boundary reconstruction），重新拟合相邻保留块以匹配原始两块输出。整个过程无需任务标签、端到端微调或额外推理参数。

Result: 在七个不同规模的Llama和Qwen模型上，于三种层移除水平下测试，XMerge在最激进的移除（k=4）时在CORE任务集上六项排名第一，在MMLU上六项排名第一（五项同时领先）。其困惑度增长远低于竞争方法。任务级置信区间显示其优势显著，且在零样本与上下文学习中始终排名前二，唯一未发生崩溃的算子。在首个校准探测中为最佳校准方法。

Conclusion: XMerge是一种高效、稳定且通用的后训练层压缩方法，无需额外参数或架构修改，可在大幅减少计算量的同时保持高模型质量，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Removing complete transformer layers preserves a standard serving architecture, but existing depth-compression methods can lose substantial quality, and the loss varies unpredictably across models. We introduce XMerge, a post-training method with two components. Cross-axis selection identifies a block with low relative-magnitude and angular hidden-state change, and local boundary reconstruction re-fits the adjacent surviving block to match the original two-block output. XMerge uses no task labels or end-to-end fine-tuning, and it introduces neither architectural changes nor additional inference-time parameters. Across seven Llama and Qwen backbones (0.5B-8B), five published baselines, and three layer-reduction levels, its advantage over baselines is largest at the most aggressive removal: at k=4 it ranks first on six of seven backbones on CORE (a 22-task aggregate) and, separately, on six of seven on MMLU (five of seven on both at once), while avoiding the large perplexity increases of several competing operators. In a task-level bootstrap, the 95% confidence intervals for the three largest CORE margins exclude zero; the remaining margins are consistent with ties. Across the 14 (model, regime) cells it is also the only evaluated operator that never collapses, ranking top-2 in both zero-shot and in-context regimes; on a first calibration probe (one backbone) it is the best-calibrated operator. Ablations show that local reconstruction provides most of the gain, while cross-axis fusion helps when the two selection axes disagree. The additional construction cost is recovered through per-token decode savings after roughly tens of thousands of requests.

</details>


### [214] [TC-Next: Zero-Shot Multimodal Cyclone Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02085)
*Zhe Wang,Sijie Chen,Yiming Luo,Daehyun Kim,Chien-Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TC-Next 是一种多模态深度学习模型，利用大气运动学和热力学场及GridSat红外卫星图像，基于GraphCast等基础模型的预报场，在6-24小时预报范围内预测热带气旋路径与强度。仅在西太平洋（WP）的GraphCast预报数据上训练，但仅依赖通用大气变量，其路径误差降低15%-44%，强度误差降低3-6倍，优于传统规则追踪器TempestExtremes；在未重新训练的情况下应用于Pangu-Weather和IFS HRES预报场，仍表现更优。零样本迁移至WeatherNext Cyclones 2025 WP季数据，其强度误差始终更低，路径误差更低或相当。消融实验表明，多模态设计在所有预报时效下均有效提升路径预测性能，并在长时效下显著改善强度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热带气旋预报模型依赖复杂规则或特定训练数据，泛化能力有限，且对多源信息融合不足。本文旨在构建一个通用、高效、可迁移的多模态深度学习框架，以提升路径与强度预报精度，尤其在长时效下表现。

Method: 提出TC-Next模型，结合大气动力热力学场与红外卫星图像作为输入，采用基于图神经网络的多模态架构，通过预训练于GraphCast数据的通用模型实现端到端预测。模型不依赖特定气象数据集或重训练，支持零样本迁移至其他天气预报系统。

Result: TC-Next在西太平洋区域上相比传统方法（TempestExtremes）路径误差降低15%-44%，强度误差降低3-6倍；在未重新训练情况下应用于Pangu-Weather和IFS HRES时仍保持优势；在2025年WP季节的WeatherNext Cyclones数据上，强度误差始终更低，路径误差相当或更优。多模态设计在所有预报时效下提升路径预测，长时效下显著增强强度预测。

Conclusion: TC-Next展示了强大的泛化能力和多模态融合优势，具备零样本迁移潜力，为热带气旋预报提供了一种高效、通用且高精度的新范式，适用于多种气象预报系统。

Abstract: We present TropicalCycloneNext (TC-Next), a multimodal deep learning model that forecasts tropical cyclone track and intensity at $6$-$24$ h leads by leveraging a foundation model's forecast fields of atmospheric kinematic and thermodynamic fields and GridSat infrared satellite imagery. Trained only on GraphCast forecasts over the Western Pacific (WP), yet reliant only on generic atmospheric variables, TC-Next on GraphCast lowers track error by $15$-$44\%$ and intensity error by a factor of $3$-$6$ relative to a conventional, rule-based tracker, TempestExtremes; applied without retraining to the forecast fields of Pangu-Weather and IFS HRES, it stays ahead of TempestExtremes on both. Applied zero-shot to the generic weather fields of WeatherNext Cyclones on the 2025 WP season, TC-Next attains lower intensity error at every lead time, and lower or comparable track error, compared to that model's specialized direct tracker in a deterministic comparison. Our ablation studies show that our multimodal model is able to utilize the additional modality to improve performance in tracking errors at every lead time and in intensity prediction at longer lead times.

</details>


### [215] [Compositional Spectral Prompts for LLM-based Online Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02093)
*Seungyoon Choi,Hyunchul Kim,Jae-Gil Lee,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoSPOT 是一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的在线时间序列预测框架，利用预训练 LLM 作为骨干模型，通过频域基底构建组合谱提示，实现高效在线适应。该方法冻结 LLM，仅更新少量参数，能有效处理长期适应和未见模式，适用于分布显著变化的跨数据集场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆缓冲区的在线时间序列预测方法在长期适应和未见模式泛化方面表现不佳，难以应对非平稳环境中的持续变化。

Method: CoSPOT 将时间序列分解为频域基底，根据各成分的振幅组合相应的谱提示，利用预训练 LLM 的少样本能力进行在线预测，同时保持模型冻结以减少参数更新量。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，CoSPOT 在长周期在线阶段和存在显著分布偏移的跨数据集设置下均表现出优越性能，具备良好的实用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CoSPOT 通过结合频域提示与预训练大语言模型，在保证高效在线学习的同时实现了对复杂、动态时间序列的有效建模，是解决在线时间序列预测挑战的一种有效新范式。

Abstract: To address the sequential and evolving nature of time series, the Online Time Series Forecasting (OTSF) task has been extensively studied in multiple domains. Existing research focuses on adapting to non-stationary environments by employing memory buffer-based retrieval strategies. However, we observe that such frameworks struggle with long-term adaptation and fail to generalize to unseen patterns. To this end, we introduce CoSPOT, an LLM-based online time series forecasting framework that leverages a pre-trained LLM as the backbone online forecaster, motivated by its strong few-shot capabilities. For efficient online adaptation, CoSPOT keeps the LLM frozen and employs compositional spectral prompts grounded in frequency-domain bases to guide the model with the overall distribution of the input, thereby substantially reducing the number of parameters updated during the online phase. Specifically, CoSPOT decomposes time series into frequency bases and composes the corresponding spectral basis prompts according to their amplitudes, allowing unseen patterns to be represented as new combinations of learned basis prompts. Our extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority and practicality of CoSPOT across challenging online scenarios, including extended online phases and cross-dataset settings with substantial distribution shifts. Our code is available at https://github.com/seungyoon-Choi/CoSPOT.

</details>


### [216] [Federated LoRA Adaptation of BiomedCLIP Across Four International Chest X-Ray Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02101)
*Sanjaya Poudel,Nirajan Kunwor,Manish Dhakal,Debesh Jha,Sunil Kumar Gaire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在异构、地理分布的医疗机构中，如何通过联邦低秩适应（Federated LoRA）对生物医学视觉-语言模型BiomedCLIP进行高效协作式微调。实验在跨三大洲四个公共队列上进行，结果表明，与未适配的预训练模型相比，联邦LoRA显著提升了所有队列的分类性能（平均AUC从0.687提升至0.802），且优于单个队列独立训练，接近集中式训练基准（0.812）。研究表明，基于奇异值分解（SVD）的产品空间聚合方法（FlexLoRA）是性能提升的关键，而传统漂移校正优化器（如FedProx）在此场景下无明显优势，说明LoRA本身已有效抑制客户端漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗影像领域，患者数据受隐私法规限制，机构间存在设备、协议和计算资源的显著异构性，传统的数据集中训练不可行。因此需要一种既能保护隐私又能有效整合多中心数据的方法。同时，随着多模态视觉-语言模型在医学图像分析中的广泛应用，如何高效地进行参数高效微调（PEFT）成为关键挑战。

Method: 采用联邦学习框架结合低秩适应（LoRA）技术，在不交换原始数据的前提下，仅传输轻量级低秩更新参数。通过在多个跨国队列上对BiomedCLIP模型进行联邦微调，评估不同聚合策略（如产品空间聚合与简单因子平均）及优化算法（如FedAvg与FedProx）的效果。

Result: 联邦LoRA显著提升了所有队列的胸片分类性能（平均AUC从0.687升至0.802），尤其改善了弱队列表现；接近集中式训练基准（0.812）。产品空间聚合（FlexLoRA）相比简单平均提升显著（降0.097 AUC），而FedProx未带来额外收益，表明LoRA的低秩特性已有效缓解客户端漂移问题。

Conclusion: 在异构、分布式医疗环境中，可通过联邦LoRA实现对生物医学视觉-语言模型的高效协作式适配，无需中央数据汇聚，既保障隐私又提升模型泛化能力，为多中心医学人工智能应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) lets institutions train a shared model without exchanging data, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) makes this practical at scale by communicating only compact low-rank updates. Biomedical imaging is a compelling setting for this combination: patient data are archived behind privacy regulations, and institutions differ widely in scanners, protocols, and compute. Such heterogeneity raises the question of how federated LoRA updates should be aggregated, increasingly pressing as multimodal vision-language models become central to medical image analysis. We benchmark federated Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of BiomedCLIP for chest radiograph classification across four public cohorts on three continents (USA, Vietnam, Spain). Federated LoRA adaptation improves shared-class AUC on all four cohorts over the unadapted BiomedCLIP backbone (mean 0.687 to 0.802), showing that the gains come from federated adaptation rather than from the pretrained model's zero-shot ability. Relative to isolated single-cohort training, federation improves the weaker cohorts while largely preserving the strongest and approaches a centralized reference (0.812) that pools all data. The singular value decomposition (SVD)-based product-space aggregation introduced by FlexLoRA is essential to this gain (naive factor averaging drops mean AUC by 0.097), whereas a drift-correcting optimizer (FedProx) shows no benefit over FedAvg in our single-seed runs, consistent with LoRA's low-rank updates already limiting client drift. Biomedical vision-language models can thus be adapted collaboratively across heterogeneous, geographically distributed institutions without centralizing data.

</details>


### [217] [A Unified Rate-Distortion Perspective on Vector, Product, and Scalar Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02107)
*Xianghong Fang,Wenlong Mou,Yuan Yuan,Dehan Kong,Tim G. J. Rudner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的率-失真视角，用于理解现代离散视觉标记化的量化权衡。通过将量化视为有损压缩，定义编码速率和量化误差，并解决了三个核心问题：最小化失真比最大化码书利用率更关键；提出了公平比较量化的两个条件（控制潜在特征统计和相同编码速率）；在这些条件下，恢复了VQ-PQ-SQ的失真层次，并实证表明现代VQ方法失真最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前离散视觉标记化缺乏统一的理论框架来理解量化过程中的权衡，特别是关于重建保真度与码书利用效率之间的关系。现有方法在评估量化器时存在模糊性，亟需一个可控制、可比较的基准。

Method: 将量化建模为有损压缩问题，引入率-失真框架，以标记数量和码书大小定义编码速率，以量化误差作为失真度量。通过理论分析与实验验证，建立公平比较的两个条件，并系统对比VQ、PQ、SQ等方法的内在性能。

Result: 理论和实证表明，最小化失真才是提升重建质量的核心目标；在控制特征统计和编码速率的前提下，现代VQ方法表现出最低的量化失真，优于PQ和SQ。该框架澄清了量化评估中的歧义，提供了在固定速率下评估量化有效性的标准化方法。

Conclusion: 本文为现代离散视觉标记化提供了一个基于率-失真的统一理论框架，解决了量化评估中的不一致问题，明确了量化性能的内在评价标准，推动了对量化方法本质的理解。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokenization, predominantly driven by vector, scalar, and product quantization, lacks a unified conceptual framework for understanding quantization tradeoffs. In this paper, we propose a unified rate--distortion perspective on modern discrete visual tokenization. By viewing quantization as lossy compression, we characterize the nominal fixed-length coding rate through token count and codebook size, and quantization error as the distortion. Within this framework, we resolve three central questions. First, we theoretically and empirically show that minimizing distortion, rather than maximizing codebook utilization, is the primary intrinsic objective for reconstruction fidelity, with a direct connection to the STE-induced gradient discrepancy. Second, we establish two critical fairness conditions for intrinsic quantization comparison: controlling latent feature statistics and enforcing identical coding rates. Third, under these conditions, we recover the VQ--PQ--SQ distortion hierarchy in modern visual tokenization and show empirically that modern VQ methods achieve the lowest distortion. This work provides a foundational rate--distortion reframing of modern discrete visual tokenization, resolves ambiguities in quantizer evaluation, and provides a controlled framework for isolating intrinsic quantization effectiveness under fixed-rate constraints.

</details>


### [218] [A Computational Comparison of Fourier Spectral Differentiation and Spatial Automatic Differentiation in Periodic Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02110)
*Xilai Liang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了物理信息神经网络（PINNs）中基于自动微分（AD）与傅里叶谱微分在周期性物理空间中的表现。在保持其他条件一致的情况下，傅里叶方法通过均匀网格上的谱变换实现空间导数计算，可重复使用傅里叶系数以高效处理高阶导数。实验涵盖标准PINNs和因果PINNs对Allen-Cahn、Korteweg-de Vries及Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程的求解，结果表明傅里叶方法在训练速度上提升2.90倍至18.52倍，GPU峰值内存减少68.7%至94.1%，且最终误差水平相当，无明显精度优势。因此，在一维周期性问题中，傅里叶谱微分显著降低计算开销并保持精度，但需依赖均匀结构化网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINNs使用自动微分计算偏微分方程残差中的空间导数，当涉及多阶或高阶导数时，其计算与内存开销显著增加。为提升效率，本文探索更高效的替代方案——傅里叶谱微分，旨在降低训练成本而不牺牲求解精度。

Method: 在固定神经网络结构、时间微分、优化器、采样策略和训练计划的前提下，对比空间自动微分与傅里叶谱微分两种空间导数计算方式。傅里叶方法将网络输出在均匀周期网格上采样后转换至频域，通过乘法快速计算各阶导数，并复用同一组傅里叶系数。实验覆盖五种不同方程-框架组合，系统评估性能差异。

Result: 傅里叶谱微分在所有测试场景中均实现显著训练加速（2.90×–18.52×），同时大幅降低GPU内存占用（68.7%–94.1%）。最终相对L2误差保持在同一数量级，未表现出系统性精度优劣。

Conclusion: 对于一维周期性问题，傅里叶谱微分相比空间自动微分能显著降低训练时间和内存消耗，且维持相当的求解精度，是一种高效可行的替代方案，但其应用受限于必须使用均匀结构化空间网格。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) commonly evaluate the spatial derivatives appearing in partial differential equation residuals using automatic differentiation (AD), whose computational and memory costs can become substantial when multiple or high-order derivatives are required. We perform a controlled comparison of spatial AD and Fourier spectral differentiation in periodic physical-space PINNs. Within each paired experiment, the neural representation, temporal differentiation, optimizer, sampling procedure, and training schedule are held fixed, so that the two cases differ only in the spatial differentiation procedure. For the Fourier variant, network outputs are evaluated on a uniform periodic grid and transformed to Fourier space, where spatial derivatives are obtained through spectral multiplication and the same Fourier coefficients are reused across derivative orders. We compare the two procedures in standard PINNs for the Allen--Cahn and Korteweg--de Vries equations and in Causal PINNs for the Allen--Cahn, Korteweg--de Vries, and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equations. Across these five equation--framework settings, Fourier differentiation yields mean paired end-to-end training speedups ranging from $2.90\times$ to $18.52\times$ and reduces peak allocated graphics processing unit (GPU) memory by $68.7\%$--$94.1\%$. The final relative $L_2$ errors remain of the same order, with neither differentiation procedure showing a consistent accuracy advantage. For the one-dimensional periodic benchmarks considered here, Fourier spectral differentiation therefore provides substantially lower training time and memory usage than spatial AD while retaining comparable solution error, at the cost of requiring a uniform structured spatial grid.

</details>


### [219] [Scalable Bayesian Optimization of Composite Functions for Image-Based Inverse Problems in Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02126)
*Dasol Yoon,Poompol Buathong,Chia-Hao Lee,Yujia Zhang,David A. Muller,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SBOCF的新型模拟高效方法，用于从电子显微图像中估计物理参数。该方法利用图像匹配目标的复合结构和模拟图像中的中间信息，通过局部块摘要和两个修正项将原始像素级目标降维至11个输出，显著减少模拟次数。在50次模拟评估预算下，相比标准贝叶斯优化，其在合成数据上将均方误差降低最多达290倍，并在实验数据上获得与已有结果一致的参数估计，无需特定任务预训练。此外，在伪晶错位样品中，使用SBOCF估计值进行后续相衬重建，恢复出清晰原子结构，证明了其在高维结构化输出与昂贵模拟场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法如网格搜索在计算上效率低下，而神经网络方法需要大量预训练且难以迁移。因此，亟需一种高效、通用且无需额外训练的逆问题求解方法，尤其适用于电子显微学中涉及昂贵物理模拟的参数估计任务。

Method: SBOCF通过将位置平均会聚束电子衍射（PACBED）图像表示为局部块摘要及两个校正项，保留原始像素级目标的同时大幅降低模型输出维度（从24,649降至11）。结合贝叶斯优化框架，利用复合函数结构和中间模拟信息，实现对复杂图像匹配目标的高效优化。

Result: 在合成的SrTiO3数据上，SBOCF在50次模拟评估下将中位数最终均方误差降低最多达290倍；在真实实验数据上，参数估计与已有文献一致，无需任务特定预训练；在伪晶错位样品中，基于SBOCF估计的参数可使相衬重构得到清晰原子结构，显著优于未优化的情况。

Conclusion: SBOCF是一种高效、可扩展的逆问题求解方法，特别适用于具有高维结构化输出和昂贵模拟器的科学图像分析任务，具备良好的泛化能力与实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Estimating physical parameters from scientific images is a common inverse problem in materials characterization that often relies on expensive physics-based simulations. In electron microscopy, specimen thickness and crystal mistilt are critical parameters that govern how electrons scatter through the sample, and therefore the accuracy of any atomic-scale structure recovered from it. They are commonly inferred by matching experimental position-averaged convergent-beam electron diffraction (PACBED) patterns to simulated ones, but grid searches scale poorly and neural-network methods require extensive pretraining that may not transfer to new conditions. Here, we propose scalable Bayesian optimization of composite functions (SBOCF), a simulation-efficient method that exploits the known composite structure of the image-matching objective and the intermediate information contained in simulated images. By representing PACBED images with patch-level summaries and two correction terms, SBOCF preserves the original pixel-wise objective while reducing the number of modeled outputs from 24,649 to 11. Under a budget of 50 simulator evaluations, SBOCF outperformed standard Bayesian optimization with expected improvement on synthetic SrTiO3 benchmarks with thick and thin specimens, reducing the median final SSE by up to 290x in the thick-sample case. On experimental data, SBOCF produced parameter estimates consistent with previously reported values without task-specific pretraining. For a simulated mistilted specimen, using the SBOCF estimates in a downstream ptychographic reconstruction recovered sharp atoms that were otherwise blurred. These results establish SBOCF as a promising approach for inverse problems involving expensive simulators and high-dimensional structured outputs.

</details>


### [220] [Online Non-Monotone DR-Submodular Maximization Matching the Offline $0.401$ Factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02145)
*Vaneet Aggarwal,Yiyang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在d维单位立方体的紧凸下闭子集上在线最大化非负、非单调的DR-子模函数。通过引入一种新的在线算法，实现了0.401的近似因子，优于以往在线环境下的1/e。算法不依赖于对变化目标的离线构造，而是采用加权在线学习器控制累积残差项，并利用不对称平衡定理保持离线系数稳定。直接实现具有O(T^{3/4})的次线性近似遗憾，每轮使用O(dT^{1/4})个预言机调用；更一般地，对于任意δ∈[0,1/4]，分批处理可实现每轮O(T^δ)调用和O(T^{4/5−δ/5})遗憾，包括单次调用的O(T^{4/5})端点。在正锚条件下，随机阻塞方法仍能保持0.401的近似因子，并达到O(T^{5/6})的一点带注释遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线算法在非单调DR-子模函数最大化问题上的近似因子仅为1/e，而离线情况下已有0.401的构造性结果。本文旨在将这一更优的离线近似因子推广到在线场景，克服动态目标带来的挑战。

Method: 提出一种基于加权在线学习器的新型在线算法，取代传统的离线目标依赖盒步操作。通过精确的不对称平衡定理，在对抗性变化下仍能维持离线最优系数。采用分批策略以调节计算与遗憾之间的权衡，并在正锚条件下引入随机阻塞机制以处理带注释反馈情形。

Result: 首次在在线设置中实现了0.401的近似因子，且在条件偏差有界的预言机反馈下，达到子线性近似遗憾。直接实现为O(T^{3/4})遗憾，每轮调用O(dT^{1/4})次预言机；分批处理可实现从O(T^{4/5})遗憾到每轮仅一次调用的灵活权衡；在正锚条件下，随机阻塞方法获得O(T^{5/6})的一点带注释遗憾。

Conclusion: 本文成功将非单调DR-子模函数在线最大化问题的近似因子提升至0.401，突破了长期停滞在1/e的瓶颈。所提算法在不同反馈模型下均表现出良好的遗憾界，为在线子模优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study online maximization of nonnegative, non-monotone DR-submodular functions over compact convex down-closed subsets of the $d$-dimensional unit cube. The best known constructive offline approximation factor is $0.401$ under the corresponding meta-solvability assumptions, whereas comparable adversarial online guarantees had remained at $1/e$. We show that this factor is also achievable online. In the post-decision full-information value-oracle model, our algorithm attains factor $0.401$ with sublinear approximate regret when oracle feedback is conditionally unbiased and bounded.
  The online algorithm does not run the offline construction on a changing objective. Instead, it replaces the offline objective-dependent box step by a weighted online learner that controls the required residual terms cumulatively. An exact asymmetric balance theorem preserves the offline coefficients despite adversarial variation. The direct implementation has $O(T^{3/4})$ regret and uses $O(dT^{1/4})$ oracle calls per round. More generally, for every $δ\in[0,1/4]$, batching gives $O(T^δ)$ calls per round and $O(T^{4/5-δ/5})$ regret, including a one-call $O(T^{4/5})$ endpoint. Under a positive-anchor condition, randomized blocking retains factor $0.401$ with $O(T^{5/6})$ one-point bandit regret.

</details>


### [221] [Exact Limits of Random Projections for Preserving Geometry: Distance Recovery, Nearest-Neighbor Rankings, and Covariance Shape in Gaussian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02155)
*Piyush Sao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了约翰逊-林登斯特拉乌斯（JL）引理在高维数据中的局限性，指出尽管JL引理保证了随机投影可保留点对间的平方距离误差在相对误差ε内，但这种保距性可能无法反映真实几何结构。作者通过分析线性压缩后的特征恢复问题，发现对于各向同性高斯数据，最优解码器的奇异值随维度比$m/d$以$(m/d)^{k/2}$衰减，表明压缩后仅能保留少量几何信息。即使满足JL条件，实际相关性可能趋近于零，说明距离保全并不等价于几何可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JL引理虽被广泛用于降维，但在高维下距离集中现象导致其保距性可能不反映真实几何信息；作者旨在揭示这种保距性与实际几何保持之间的脱节，量化压缩后可恢复的几何特征能力。

Method: 构建线性压缩模型，分析从压缩数据中恢复原始点间平方距离特征的最优解码器，利用条件期望作为损失函数下的最优估计，并针对各向同性高斯数据推导出解码算子的奇异值解析表达式，进而分析其渐近行为。

Result: 对于固定秩$k$，第$k$个奇异值满足$\ell_k \approx (m/d)^{k/2}$；当$m,d-m\to\infty$时，压缩后最多保留$\frac{m}{d}$的方差；若$m\to\infty$且$m/d\to0$，预期肯德尔相关系数为$\frac{2}{\pi}\sqrt{m/d}(1+o(1))$，最近邻一致性趋于$1/q$；而即便满足JL条件，平均肯德尔相关仍可趋近于零。压缩后保留的协方差形状信息约为$(m/d)^2$。

Conclusion: JL引理所保证的距离保全并不能反映实际可用于比较或推断的几何结构；压缩后的真实几何信息远低于引理所暗示的程度，因此应谨慎依赖该引理作为几何保持的指标。

Abstract: The Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) lemma guarantees that a random projection of $n$ points to
  $m=O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log n)$ dimensions preserves pairwise squared distances within relative
  error $\varepsilon$ with high probability, and this dimension order is asymptotically
  optimal. In high dimensions, however, distances concentrate around a baseline while key
  geometric information lies in much smaller fluctuations. We show that the JL bound can
  therefore be uninformative about retained geometry: an independent Gaussian replacement map
  can satisfy it even though the replacement cloud is independent of the original data.
  We then ask how well any decoder can recover a feature $f(D)$ of a squared distance $D$ from
  a linear sketch. Under squared-error loss, the optimal decoder is conditional expectation, so
  recovery defines a linear operator whose singular values quantify feature recovery. For
  isotropic Gaussian data ($Σ=σ^2 I_d$), we diagonalize this operator in closed form.
  For fixed $k$ with $m,d-m\to\infty$, its $k$th singular value satisfies $\ell_k\approx(m/
  d)^{k/2}$.
  This yields three sharp consequences. A rank-$m$ sketch retains at most an $m/d$ fraction of
  the variance of any feature of one squared distance. If $m\to\infty$ and $m/d\to0$, the
  expected Kendall correlation is $\frac{2}π\sqrt{m/d}(1+o(1))$; for fixed $q$, nearest-
  neighbor agreement tends to $1/q$. Yet one projection can satisfy the JL bound while mean
  Kendall correlation vanishes when $\log n\ll m\ll d$. After removing scale, Haar-averaged
  retained covariance-shape information is $(m/d)^2$. Thus JL distance preservation does not
  quantify the geometry available for comparison or inference.

</details>


### [222] [GeoSPRINT: Geometric Redundancy-Aware Step Pruning for Inference in Diffusion Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02160)
*Arpita Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoSPRINT 是一种无需训练的框架，通过分析去噪轨迹的几何结构来构建非均匀采样调度。它利用潜空间中的超平面性测试（通过 QR 分解高效实现）检测几何冗余步骤，并将冗余分布转化为采样调度，使更多步骤集中在轨迹高曲率区域。同时引入轨迹投影得分 $\alpha_{\mathrm{traj}}$ 作为模型无关的诊断指标，衡量轨迹直线度。在 CIFAR-10、LSUN Church 和 Stable Diffusion v1.5 上，GeoSPRINT 在相同 NFE 预算下均优于均匀 DDIM 调度，显著提升生成质量（如 FID 改善 0.7–1.1），并超越 DPM-Solver++，证明轨迹几何可有效指导采样效率优化，且无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型加速方法存在缺陷：固定步长跳过、基于局部误差自适应步长或需额外训练。本文旨在提出一种无需训练的高效采样策略，利用去噪轨迹的全局几何信息优化采样步骤分配，以提升推理效率和生成质量。

Method: GeoSPRINT 通过在潜空间中使用超平面性测试检测几何冗余步骤，结合 QR 因子分解实现高效计算；基于冗余分布生成非均匀采样调度，优先在高曲率区域分配更多步骤；引入轨迹投影得分 $\alpha_{\mathrm{traj}}$ 作为模型无关的轨迹直线度评估指标，用于诊断修正流质量。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10（32×32）、LSUN Church（256×256）和 Stable Diffusion v1.5（512×512 潜空间）上，GeoSPRINT 均显著优于统一的 DDIM 调度，在相同 NFE 预算下降低 FID 0.7–1.1，且在 NFE ≥30 时超越 DPM-Solver++；在 LSUN Church 上将 FID 从 1.48 降至 1.26（52 步），在 Stable Diffusion 中实现最高 1.93 的 FID 改进。

Conclusion: 轨迹几何提供了可用于优化采样步骤分配的有用全局信号，通过 GeoSPRINT 可显著提升扩散模型推理效率，无需重新训练即可实现高质量生成，为高效扩散采样提供新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve high sample quality but remain expensive at inference time because sampling requires many sequential neural function evaluations (NFEs). Existing acceleration methods either use fixed step-skipping schedules, adapt step sizes based on local numerical error, or require additional training. We introduce GeoSPRINT (Geometric Step Pruning for Inference in Trajectories), a training-free framework for constructing non-uniform sampling schedules from the geometry of denoising trajectories. GeoSPRINT detects geometrically redundant steps using a hyperplanarity test in latent space, implemented efficiently via QR factorization, and converts the resulting redundancy profile into a sampling schedule that allocates more steps to high-curvature regions of the trajectory. In addition, we introduce the trajectory projection score $α_{\mathrm{traj}}$, a residual-variance metric that quantifies trajectory straightness and serves as a model-free diagnostic for rectified flow quality. Across CIFAR-10 ($32{\times}32$), LSUN Church ($256{\times}256$), and Stable Diffusion v1.5 ($512{\times}512$ latent), GeoSPRINT consistently improves over uniform DDIM (Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models) schedules at matched NFE budgets. On CIFAR-10, GeoSPRINT improves FID (Fréchet Inception Distance) by 0.7-1.1 over DDIM across 49-89 NFEs and surpasses DPM-Solver++ at NFE${\geq}30$ despite using a first-order DDIM solver. On LSUN Church, it reduces FID from 1.48 to 1.26 at 52 steps, and on Stable Diffusion v1.5 it achieves up to 1.93 FID improvement over DDIM. These results show that trajectory geometry provides a useful global signal for allocating inference steps and that schedule quality can substantially improve diffusion sampling efficiency without retraining.

</details>


### [223] [DMRL: Document-Mediated Reinforcement Learning for Skill Optimization in Advertising Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02170)
*Wei Zhang,Hongji Li,Song Sun,Peng Yu,Xue Yang,Lei Zhao,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为文档中介强化学习（DMRL）的技能自演化框架，用于广告推荐中的复杂系统参数调优。该框架将技能文档优化建模为一系列结构化编辑动作，通过上层代理进行受控编辑，下层任务代理通过A/B测试评估效果。为解决信用分配和长期收益问题，引入了双相对策略优化（DRPO）和长期奖励预测器（LRP），分别实现稳健的风险感知优势估计和基于解耦表示学习与跨注意力迁移的长期结果预测。在大规模短视频广告平台上的实证评估表明，DMRL在关键广告指标上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前广告推荐系统依赖人工调整复杂参数，而基于大语言模型的技能优化多为提示驱动，缺乏对具体文档修改的奖励归因机制，难以实现高效、可解释的自演化优化。

Method: DMRL框架包含上层编辑代理与冻结的下层任务代理，通过结构化编辑动作优化技能文档；采用双相对策略优化（DRPO）进行后训练策略优化以提升优势估计的鲁棒性；利用长期奖励预测器（LRP）结合解耦表示学习与跨注意力机制，建模用户群体异质性并预测长期效果。

Result: 在大规模短视频广告平台上的实验显示，DMRL显著优于现有先进基线，在多个关键广告指标（如点击率、转化率等）上均取得提升，具备良好的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: DMRL通过结构化文档编辑与创新的信用分配机制，实现了广告技能的自主优化，为平衡商业收益与用户体验提供了可扩展、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Advertising recommendation requires continuously tuning complex system parameters while balancing commercial returns and user experience. Recent work has introduced large language models (LLMs) with skill documents to assist this labor-intensive process, but skill optimization remains largely prompt-driven, lacking a principled mechanism to attribute rewards to specific document edits. To address this limitation, we propose Document-Mediated Reinforcement Learning (DMRL), a skill self-evolution framework that models skill document optimization as a sequence of structured editing actions. In DMRL, an upper-level agent performs controlled document edits, while a frozen lower-level task agent evaluates their effects through A/B testing. To address credit assignment and long-term outcomes, we introduce two key components: (1) Dual-Relative Policy Optimization (DRPO), a post-training policy optimization method for robust and risk-aware advantage estimation; and (2) Long-term Reward Predictor (LRP), which estimates long-term outcomes by modeling population heterogeneity with disentangled representation learning and cross-attention transfer. DMRL was deployed on a large-scale short-video ads platform and extensive empirical evaluation shows that DMRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across key advertising metrics

</details>


### [224] [Learning the Constitutive Behavior of Materials via Neural Operators and Causal Attention: Case Studies in Plasticity and Damage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02194)
*Rishabh Arora,Lisa Scheunemann,Tim Brepols,Shahed Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于材料算子的数据驱动本构建模框架，将材料视为从应变历史到应力响应的函数映射。通过因果掩码注意力机制实现时间路径依赖，同时保持计算并行性；利用谱卷积实现频域上的离散化不变表示，并采用正弦激活函数捕捉非弹性阶段的强非线性行为。该方法在多维、率无关材料模型上表现优异，能准确预测非弹性变形机制，具备分辨率不变性和良好的并行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本构模型依赖不可测的内变量和经验假设，难以仅从应力-应变数据中推断材料行为。本文旨在开发一种无需先验内变量知识、直接从完整加载路径学习应力响应的端到端数据驱动框架。

Method: 构建基于材料算子的函数到函数映射模型，采用因果掩码注意力机制编码历史依赖，结合谱卷积实现频率域的离散化不变表示，使用正弦激活函数处理强非线性，通过单次前向传播预测完整应力轨迹。

Result: 在复杂非线性塑性和延性损伤累积等多维材料模型上验证，模型表现出高精度与鲁棒性，具备分辨率不变性及优异的并行计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提数据驱动框架能够有效建模路径依赖的非弹性材料行为，无需内变量假设，可直接从测量的应变-应力数据中学习本构关系，具有良好的泛化能力与计算效率。

Abstract: Classical constitutive modeling of path-dependent inelastic materials relies on internal state variables whose evolution equations must be postulated based on domain knowledge and calibrated against experimental data. However, in many practical settings, the relevant internal variables are typically not measurable in experiments, and the constitutive response must be inferred entirely from measured strain-stress data without any prior knowledge of the material's internal state. We propose a data-driven constitutive modeling framework based on the concept of a material operator, which treats a deforming material as a functional mapping from its entire strain history to the corresponding stress response. In contrast to traditional autoregressive or recurrent formulations, the model is trained directly on full loading paths as function-to-function mappings, predicting complete stress trajectories in a single parallel forward pass. Temporal path dependence is enforced through a causally masked attention mechanism embedded within the operator, which restricts the model's attention to past material states while preserving computational parallelizability. Spectral convolutions provide discretization-invariant representations in the frequency domain, while causal attention captures highly adaptive, non-local history dependence. Furthermore, sinusoidal activation functions are used to resolve the strong nonlinear transitions inherent in inelastic regimes. The framework is evaluated across multidimensional, rate-independent material models exhibiting complex phenomena, with an emphasis on nonlinear plasticity and ductile damage accumulation. The results demonstrate accurate and robust predictions of irreversible deformation mechanisms while simultaneously achieving resolution invariance and excellent parallel efficiency.

</details>


### [225] [SMart: A Multi-source Multi-phase Time Series Representation Transfer Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02203)
*Fang He,Wang-chien Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SMart的新时间序列表示学习框架，通过引入多阶段递归图恢复任务和多源数据集选择器，解决了现有方法在自监督恢复任务简单和仅使用单一源数据集的局限性。实验表明，SMart在多种时间序列任务中表现优异，相比现有先进模型，分类准确率平均提升1.34%，回归任务的平均绝对误差降低最多达19.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列表示学习方法在自监督恢复任务上设计较为简单，且通常仅依赖单一目标数据集进行预训练，限制了表示能力的提升。因此需要更复杂的恢复任务和多源数据支持以增强模型泛化性和表征能力。

Method: 提出多阶段递归图恢复任务（三种不同模式），用于引导编码器捕捉时间序列动态特征；设计源数据集选择器，从多个潜在源数据集中筛选适合的目标数据集，用于预训练编码器。

Result: SMart在单变量和多变量时间序列数据集上的分类和回归任务中均优于多个当前最先进的模型，分类任务平均准确率提升1.34%，回归任务平均绝对误差降低最多达19.5%。

Conclusion: SMart通过引入多阶段恢复任务和多源数据选择机制，显著提升了时间序列表示学习的效果，验证了多源、多阶段自监督学习在时间序列建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Time series representation learning (TSRL) has attracted growing research interests in recent years. Two recent explorations in TSRL are: i) exploiting a transformer-based framework to learn time series; ii) instead of using only the targeted dataset, borrowing time series from other datasets to to facilitate representation transfer. While these two explorations are shown effective, the self-supervised time series recovery task in (i) and the single-source dataset used in (ii) are technically simple and thus can be enhanced with new ideas. In this work, we propose a new TSRL framework, namely multi-source multi-phase time series representation transfer (SMart), which has two novel mechanisms to address the aforementioned deficiencies: 1) a multi-phase recurrence plots recovery task, in three alternative modes, for guiding the encoder to embed time series dynamics into the time series representation; and 2) a source dataset selector to select multiple suitable source datasets to supplement the original target dataset for pre-training the TSRL encoder. Experimental results show that SMart outperforms several state-of-the-art models for time series representation learning, classification and regression on both uni-variate and multi-variate time series datasets, reducing mean absolute error up to 19.5% for time series regression, and increasing average accuracy up to 1.34\% for time series classification.

</details>


### [226] [Recursive Value Learning for Long-Horizon Offline Goal-Conditioned RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02237)
*Hyeonseong Jeon,Youngwoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DCRL proposes a divide-and-conquer approach for offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning by recursively decomposing trajectories into a balanced binary tree, enabling value updates from leaves to root. This reduces error propagation and bootstrap depth from linear to logarithmic, improving performance on long-horizon tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scaling offline GCRL to long-horizon tasks is challenging due to inaccurate short-range value estimates and overestimation amplification via max-based backups.

Method: DCRL constructs a balanced binary tree over trajectory segments, trains values bottom-up using exact route factorization, and jointly propagates values across trajectories to discover shorter routes.

Result: DCRL outperforms prior flat offline GCRL methods; on five challenging OGBench tasks, it improves average score from 55 to 64, surpassing all flat and hierarchical baselines.

Conclusion: By leveraging a balanced binary tree structure for value learning, DCRL effectively mitigates error accumulation and enables scalable, high-performance offline goal-conditioned RL for long-horizon tasks.

Abstract: Scaling offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) to long-horizon tasks is difficult because (1) long-range value learning depends on shorter-range estimates that may still be inaccurate, and (2) max-based value backups can amplify overestimation through repeated propagation. We propose DCRL (Divide-and-Conquer RL), which recursively decomposes each trajectory segment into a balanced binary tree and trains the values from leaves to root. Each parent is therefore updated only after its children, using an exact factorization of the observed route rather than selecting among noisy alternatives. Since this objective learns values along demonstrated routes that are not necessarily optimal, DCRL jointly propagates values across trajectories to discover shorter routes. Thanks to the balanced binary tree, DCRL reduces worst-case bootstrap depth from linear to logarithmic, and this shorter dependency structure empirically corresponds to much slower error accumulation. Across diverse goal-reaching tasks, DCRL substantially outperforms prior flat offline GCRL methods, and on the five most challenging long-horizon OGBench tasks, it improves the best prior average score from 55 to 64, surpassing all flat and hierarchical baselines.

</details>


### [227] [Similarity-Aware Personalized Federated Learning in Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02241)
*Arun Kumar A,Sunil Gupta,Dang Ngyuen,Bao Duong,Dat Phan Trong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAPE-FL是一种新颖的个性化联邦学习框架，通过将每个客户端的模型同时锚定在全局模型和相似性加权的同伴平均模型上，有效缓解了客户端间数据分布不匹配导致的性能下降问题。该方法利用基于模型相似性和输出相似性的动态、客户端特定正则化，自适应地平衡全局知识迁移与同伴协作，同时过滤不相似的客户端，从而减少负向迁移，在高统计异质性和低客户端数据条件下显著优于现有先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端间的数据分布差异常导致全局模型泛化能力差且本地性能下降，部分仅使用本地数据训练的客户端模型甚至优于全局模型，削弱了协同学习的优势。因此需要一种能够兼顾全局知识共享与个性化需求的方法。

Method: 提出SAPE-FL框架，通过双重锚定机制（全局模型与相似性加权的同伴平均模型）结合动态正则化策略，利用模型和输出相似性评估客户端间的相关性，实现自适应的知识融合与负向迁移抑制。

Result: 理论分析证明了算法的收敛性；实验表明，在高统计异质性和低客户端数据场景下，SAPE-FL显著优于当前最先进的方法，提升了全局与本地性能。

Conclusion: SAPE-FL通过相似性感知的双锚定机制，有效应对联邦学习中的异质性挑战，增强了模型鲁棒性与个性化能力，为实际应用提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) allows decentralized clients to train models collaboratively while preserving data privacy. However, distribution mismatch across clients often leads to poor global generalization and degraded local client-level performance. In such scenarios, some of the clients with their local models trained solely on local data may perform better than the globally learnt model, thus nullifying the benefits of collaborative federated learning. To address this, we propose SAPE-FL (Similarity-Aware Personalized Federated Learning), a novel personalization framework that anchors each client's model to both the global model and a similarity-weighted peer averaged model. By incorporating dynamic, client-specific regularization based on both model similarity and output similarity, SAPE-FL adaptively balances global knowledge transfer and peer collaboration while filtering out dissimilar clients. This dual anchoring mitigates negative transfer and enhances robustness in heterogeneous settings. We theoretically analyze our algorithm establishing its convergence guarantees and empirically show that SAPE-FL outperforms state-of-the-art methods under high statistical heterogeneity and low client data regimes.

</details>


### [228] [CAPTURE: Disentangling Preference Drift from Memory Poisoning in Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02265)
*S M Asif Hossain,Ruksat Khan Shayoni,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAPTURE 是一种用于增强个性化语言代理安全性的新框架，通过神经微分方程信念追踪、多时间尺度记忆账本、不确定性触发的澄清以及反事实审计来解决偏好真实性判断问题。在 480 个用户测试集上，其胜率达 71.5%，优于基线模型，并能有效抵御记忆投毒攻击，同时接受 83.5% 的真实偏好更新。然而，在面对具备模型权重访问能力的自适应攻击者时，攻击成功率升至 24.7%，揭示了适应性与安全性之间的权衡。零样本评估和长期交互回放验证了该方法在真实场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言代理依赖持久记忆进行用户适配，但该机制也易受对抗性记忆污染攻击。当新信息与已有偏好冲突时，系统难以区分真实偏好漂移、上下文临时变化或恶意篡改，因此亟需一种能够准确判断偏好真实性的机制。

Method: 提出 CAPTURE 框架，包含：(1) 基于神经微分方程的信念追踪器以建模用户状态演化；(2) 多时间尺度记忆账本以管理不同可信度的信息；(3) 不确定性触发的澄清机制，主动询问用户以确认模糊输入；(4) 反事实审计，检验引用记忆是否影响决策合理性。

Result: 在 480 个独立用户测试片段中，CAPTURE 胜率为 71.5%，优于相同监督训练的基线（69.3%）和最强启发式基线（66.1%）。对固定策略攻击的防御成功率高达 88.5%，且接受 83.5% 的真实偏好更新。但在自适应攻击下，攻击成功率上升至 24.7%，表明存在适应性与安全性之间的权衡。零样本评估与长期历史重放验证显示其泛化能力良好。

Conclusion: 显式建模偏好真实性可显著提升记忆增强型大语言模型代理的个性化表现与系统鲁棒性，但需正视适应性与安全性的内在矛盾，未来工作应探索更高效的防御机制。

Abstract: Personalized language agents use persistent memory to adapt to users over time, but the same mechanism creates an attack surface. When new information conflicts with stored preferences, an agent must distinguish genuine preference drift from temporary context shifts, ambiguity, or adversarial memory poisoning. We formulate this problem as a continuous-time partially observable decision process over a latent user state and show why rules based only on recency and provenance are insufficient. CAPTURE addresses this ambiguity with a neural differential-equation belief tracker, a multi-timescale memory ledger, uncertainty-triggered clarification, and counterfactual auditing of cited memories. On 480 held-out episodes from 96 users, CAPTURE achieves a 71.5% win rate, compared with 69.3% for an identically supervised baseline and 66.1% for the strongest heuristic baseline. It limits fixed-policy poisoning success to 11.5% while accepting 83.5% of genuine preference updates. Under an adaptive attacker with access to the released weights, attack success rises to 24.7%, exposing a real adaptation-security tradeoff. We further evaluate the frozen system zero-shot on an independently constructed benchmark and replay longitudinal interaction histories from 40 users collected over two to three weeks. These results suggest that modeling preference authenticity explicitly can improve both personalization and robustness in memory-augmented LLM agents.

</details>


### [229] [Entangled Representations Amplify Collateral Damage in Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02285)
*Evžen Wybitul,Tim G. J. Rudner,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过控制实验验证了可解释性研究中的一个长期直觉：表征纠缠会增加遗忘的难度。研究利用选择性梯度掩码（SGTM）训练了一系列参数为254M的语言模型，这些模型在生物学与非生物学知识之间的解耦程度不同。对每个模型应用三种标准的遗忘方法后发现，解耦程度更高的模型在保留-遗忘权衡上表现更优：在固定遗忘水平下，最解耦的模型在两种方法中保留成本降低约4倍，第三种方法中降低1.3倍。由于干预仅改变模型结构，不涉及数据或遗忘算法，这直接证明了表征纠缠是导致遗忘过程中附带损害的原因之一。该设计也可用于检验其他可解释性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，研究者认为神经网络中不同知识领域间的表征纠缠会增加遗忘的难度，但这一假设尚未经过受控实验验证。本文旨在通过实证手段检验这一直觉。

Method: 使用选择性梯度掩码（SGTM）训练六组254M参数的语言模型，系统地调节其在生物学与非生物学知识之间的解耦程度；随后应用三种标准遗忘方法评估各模型的表现，并比较保留与遗忘之间的权衡。

Result: 解耦程度更高的模型在遗忘任务中表现出显著更优的保留-遗忘平衡。在固定遗忘水平下，最解耦模型的保留成本比最纠缠模型低1.3至4倍，表明表征纠缠确实加剧了遗忘过程中的附带损害。

Conclusion: 表征纠缠是导致遗忘过程中产生附带损害的重要原因，这一发现为可解释性研究中的长期直觉提供了直接实验证据。该实验设计可推广至检验其他结构化假设。

Abstract: A long-held intuition in interpretability research is that representational entanglement, the sharing of structure between knowledge domains in a neural network, makes unlearning harder. While the intuition is widespread, it has never been directly tested in a controlled experiment. We present a way to do so: by repurposing Selective Gradient Masking (SGTM), we train a suite of six 254M-parameter language models on English Wikipedia with graded levels of disentanglement between biology and non-biology knowledge. Applying three standard unlearning methods to every model in the suite, we find that more disentangled models consistently achieve better retain-forget trade-offs: at a fixed level of forgetting, the most disentangled models incur roughly $4\times$ lower retain cost under two of the three methods, and $1.3\times$ lower under the third. Because our intervention changes only the model, not the data or the unlearning algorithm, this is direct evidence that representational entanglement is one of the causes of collateral damage in unlearning, as interpretability researchers have long suspected. A similar design could be used to test other structural claims from interpretability.

</details>


### [230] [SEAL: Reinforcing Global Safety in Mixture-of-Experts through Shared Expert ALignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02293)
*Qingyu Meng,Yiwei Zha,Jiahuan Pei,Koen Hindriks,Herbert Bos,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEAL和SEAL++两种基于共享专家的防御机制，以应对Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型在对抗攻击下的脆弱性问题。通过利用始终激活的共享专家作为路由无关的安全锚点，显著提升模型全局安全对齐能力。SEAL在训练阶段实现参数高效，且可即插即用；SEAL++引入正交约束，保留原有安全子空间。实验表明，SEAL可将攻击成功率降低最高达60%，同时性能损失不超过1.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽具备高效扩展能力，但其稀疏路由机制易受对抗攻击影响，如通过恶意提示、微调或神经元剪枝操控专家选择路径，导致安全失效。现有防御多集中于加固路由器，但难以应对路由过程的不确定性，因此亟需一种不依赖路由路径的鲁棒安全机制。

Method: 提出SEAL与SEAL++：利用始终激活的共享专家中包含的安全关键神经元，构建独立于路由决策的全局安全锚点；通过训练阶段添加轻量级适配器实现参数高效防御，并在SEAL++中引入正交约束以保持原有安全表示空间。

Result: 在六种攻击场景下，SEAL将攻击成功率（ASR）平均降低60%，性能下降控制在1.4%以内；且支持与路由器级防御无缝集成，具有良好的实用性与兼容性。

Conclusion: 共享专家可作为稳定、可靠的全局安全锚点，突破稀疏路由带来的不确定性限制。SEAL及其变体为MoE模型提供了有效、低成本、可部署的安全增强方案，显著提升了模型在复杂攻击下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a scaling architecture for large language models that activates only a small subset of expert modules per token, enabling massive parameter growth with nearly constant computation. Recent Hybrid MoE architecture adds \textit{shared experts} to capture consistently useful representations, further improving stability and generalization. MoE now powers many flagship open-source and commercial models, yet remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Specifically, sparse routing introduces a structural vulnerability: MoE safety hinges on which experts are activated, and adversaries can subvert this selection through jailbreak prompts, malicious fine-tuning, and weight-level pruning of safety-critical neurons. Existing defenses primarily focus on hardening the router, but an adversary may still manipulate or bypass the routing trajectory due to the routing process's nondeterministic nature, thereby collapsing the defense. To cope with this problem, we first identify theoretically and empirically that shared expert, an always-activated component containing a small proportion of safety-critical neurons, can overcome the uncertainty of sparsely activated routing path and serve as a router-independent anchor to enhance global safety alignment. Based on this insight, we propose SEAL, a training-time parameter-efficient defense that produces a plug-and-play adapter attached to shared expert, and SEAL++, a variant that adds an orthogonal constraint preserving pre-existing safety subspaces during training. We evaluate SEAL and SEAL++ across six attack scenarios that combine three adversarial inputs (harmful prompting, jailbreak, malicious fine-tuning) with and without neuron pruning. SEAL reduces attack success rate (ASR) by up to 60\%, at a capability cost of at most 1.4\% on a five-benchmark average. Additionally, SEAL can seamlessly integrate with router-level ......

</details>


### [231] [Bayes-Optimal BER and AUC: Estimation and Evaluation of Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02304)
*Ryota Ushio,Takashi Ishida,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于软标签的最优平衡错误率（BER）和受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUC）估计方法，解决了在类别不平衡或标注噪声场景下传统准确率指标不适用的问题。研究分两部分：一是估计，提出在已知真实软标签与类别先验的干净情形下的估计器，并扩展到更现实的场景——类别先验未知且观测软标签受未知保序变换及加性噪声影响，通过等序回归与截断均值估计恢复并构建有限样本误差界；二是评估，将原用于贝叶斯误差估计的FeeBee框架拓展至最优BER和AUC，实现无需真实最优值即可对估计器性能进行实际评估。实验在合成与真实数据集上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在类别严重不平衡或标注存在噪声的场景中，传统准确率无法有效反映模型性能，而平衡错误率（BER）和AUC等指标更为合适。然而，这些指标的最优值难以估计，缺乏有效的估计与评估方法，因此亟需一种基于软标签的可计算、可评估的最优BER与AUC估计框架。

Method: 提出基于软标签的最优BER与AUC估计器，采用等序回归恢复清洁软标签，用截断均值估计类别先验，在不确定条件下构建插件估计器，并推导其有限样本误差界；同时扩展FeeBee框架以支持对最优BER与AUC估计器的无真值评估。

Result: 所提估计器在合成与真实数据集上表现良好，能够有效估计最优BER与AUC；新提出的评估框架无需真实最优值即可提供可靠的评价分数，适用于任意此类估计器。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了基于软标签的最优BER与AUC估计与评估体系，为复杂场景下的模型性能分析提供了有效工具，填补了现有方法在非理想条件下的空白。

Abstract: A fundamental quantity in machine learning is the optimal performance achievable by any model on a given task. Estimating this quantity allows us to distinguish the irreducible part of the error from a deficiency of the model, telling us how much room for improvement remains. Recent work has shown that the Bayes error, or equivalently the optimal accuracy, can be estimated from soft labels in binary classification. However, accuracy is often a poor summary of performance in settings with severe class imbalance or noisy annotations, where metrics such as the balanced error rate (BER) and the area under the ROC curve (AUC) are more appropriate. We address this gap with two complementary contributions. (i) Estimation. We propose soft-label-based estimators for the optimal BER and AUC. We first consider the clean setting in which true soft labels and the class prior are known, and then extend the estimators to a more realistic setting in which the class prior is unknown and the observed soft labels are corrupted by an unknown order-preserving transformation, possibly followed by additive noise. In the latter setting, we approximately recover the clean soft labels via isotonic regression with auxiliary hard labels, estimate the class prior with a clipped mean of the hard labels, and derive finite-sample error bounds for the resulting plug-in estimators. (ii) Evaluation. Since the optimum is unobservable on real datasets, evaluating any such estimator is itself nontrivial. We extend the FeeBee framework, originally proposed for evaluating Bayes-error estimators, to the optimal BER and AUC. The resulting procedure provides practical evaluation scores without requiring knowledge of the optimum, and applies to any estimator of the optimal BER or AUC, not only our proposed ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate both the estimators and the evaluation procedure.

</details>


### [232] [What Is Worth Representing? Representational Empowerment for Continual Model Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02322)
*Fei Dai,Hanqi Zhou,Alison Gopnik,Charley Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代表性赋能（RepEmp）作为持续模型构建的机制，用于评估可复用的表征元素的价值，强调在有限资源下决定建模什么、保留什么和重用什么。通过分层的策划者-执行者架构，在因果学习和开放词汇规划任务中验证了其有效性，结果表明RepEmp能促进结构恢复、跨任务迁移和模型压缩，优于基于信息增益的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型构建不仅关注参数或因果结构的估计，更关键的是决定哪些内容应被表征。现有方法缺乏对表征选择的系统性指导，尤其在资源受限条件下难以持续构建有效模型。因此需要一种机制来指导代理在不断变化的环境中选择、保留和重用表征元素。

Method: 提出代表性赋能（RepEmp），将赋能定义为对内部表征的控制能力，而非外部状态；设计分层的策划者-执行者架构，其中策划者根据RepEmp评分筛选和构建表征库，执行者利用该库进行建模与规划；在三个实验中测试该框架：闭合词汇因果学习、人类建模行为匹配模拟、开放词汇规划中的LLM增强型策划者。

Result: 在闭合词汇任务中，人类参与者倾向于以目标可达性为目标构造抽象因果模型，这一行为更符合RepEmp预测而非信息增益；模拟结果显示，由RepEmp引导的建模显著提升结构恢复与跨任务迁移性能；在开放词汇规划中，使用LLM的策划者构建出更紧凑且泛化能力更强的符号库，而移除RepEmp则导致性能下降。

Conclusion: RepEmp是持续模型构建的核心原则，能够有效指导代理在资源受限情况下做出关于表征构建、保留与复用的智能决策，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: The first problem of modeling the world is not just estimating the right parameters or causal structure, but deciding what should be represented at all. We frame this problem as continual model construction: an agent maintains an environment-specific model M of an inaccessible world W and curates a persistent library L of reusable representational elements across environments. We propose Representational Empowerment (RepEmp) to score candidate elements by how much they expand the agent's future capacity to model and plan, complementing the classic definition of empowerment, but redefined as control over internal representations instead of external states. We realize the framework as a hierarchical Curator-Actor architecture and test it across three experiments.
